Motivation: 在结构化元数据检索中，记录序列化时字段顺序的选择通常被视为实现细节，但作者发现微调编码器后模型会依赖字段的绝对位置而非字段标签，导致在不同字段顺序下重建索引时检索质量大幅下降。此外，传统使用日志无法为无人搜索过的指标提供训练信号，因此需要自动生成查询来训练模型。

Method: 提出排列不变微调（PI-FT），在数据加载器中为每条记录随机采样新的字段顺序并随机丢弃字段，使得模型将语义绑定到字段标签而非位置。同时构建DevDataBench基准，利用LLM生成覆盖15种语言、面向所有指标的事实性多面查询，用于训练和评估。

Result: 标准微调在字段顺序变化下损失7.4 nDCG@10点；PI-FT将惩罚降至0.2点，且几乎不损失分布内精度。微调后的118M参数CPU编码器在所有零样本基线中表现最佳，nDCG@10达到0.707，远超text-embedding-3-large的0.556，在低资源语言上提升最大。

Conclusion: 字段顺序是结构化元数据检索中不可忽视的关键因素，PI-FT有效解决了位置偏差问题。DevDataBench为多语言、多面的统计指标检索提供了训练和评估基准。随着AI助手和智能体越来越多地作为公共数据访问的中介，检索质量决定了答案是否基于正确的指标，因此该工作对通过AI传播数据具有前提性意义。

Abstract: We study retrieval over catalogs of structured metadata, where each record is a small schema whose fields answer different kinds of query. Embedding a record with a text encoder first serializes its fields into a string, which forces a choice of field order. We show this choice, usually treated as an implementation detail, silently controls retrieval quality once the encoder is fine-tuned. A standard fine-tune loses 7.4 nDCG@10 points when the index is rebuilt under a different field order, because it reads absolute position instead of the field labels. We propose permutation-invariant fine-tuning ($\textbf{PI-FT}$), which serializes each record under a freshly sampled field order with random field dropout, so meaning binds to the labels rather than to position. The change is about two lines in the data loader; it costs negligible in-distribution accuracy and cuts the order-change penalty to 0.2 points. We study this in the discovery of development statistics, a catalog of nearly 10,000 indicators that should be searchable in many languages by a model small enough to self-host. As AI assistants and agents increasingly mediate access to public data and statistics, this retrieval step decides whether an answer is grounded in the right indicator or series, making discoverability a precondition for disseminating data through AI. Because usage logs cannot provide training signal for indicators no one has searched, we generate the queries instead. $\textbf{DevDataBench}$ is a fully LLM-generated benchmark of grounded, facet-targeted queries across 15 languages, covering every indicator for both training and evaluation. A fine-tuned 118M-parameter CPU encoder outperforms every zero-shot baseline, including $\texttt{text-embedding-3-large}$ (0.707 vs.\ 0.556 nDCG@10), with the largest gains in low-resource languages. We release the benchmark, pipeline, models, and a reusable PI-FT framework.

</details>


### [111] [SIMAX: A Scalable and Interpretable Framework for Multi-Fidelity and Annotated Clinician-Patient Dialogue Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30491)
*Zhuhan Bao,Rui Yang,Bohao Yang,Zhiyi Liu,Sicheng Shu,Ruio Heerschap,Le Li,Doris Yang,Elisabeth Bond,Haoyuan Wang,Nicoleta Economou-Zavlanos,Joshua M. Biro,Matthew McDermott,Nan Liu,Anand Chowdhury,Kai Sun,Kathryn Pollak,Ed Hammond,Chuan Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SIMAX是一个用于生成受控临床对话数据并带有参考行为标注的框架，能够生成3,388个跨三个专科的模拟医患对话，经自动和人工评估显示出合理的语音自然度、高转录保真度和一定的临床真实感，可用于开发和验证沟通编码系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境数字抄录员的大规模部署驱动了医患对话的广泛采集，但人工编码临床沟通数据成本高、一致性差且难以扩展，因此需要AI驱动的沟通编码系统。然而评估这类系统需要真实对话数据和人工编码标签，两者都难以大规模获取，这促使了SIMAX框架的开发。

Method: SIMAX从预定义的临床场景、人物角色、语音条件及目标沟通行为出发生成医患对话。行为控制通过两个编码本实现：Global Codebook用于整体沟通质量，WISER Codebook用于特定可计数行为。采用自动评估（UTMOS、WV-MOS、WER、CER、CLAP余弦相似度）和人工评估（MOS、临床真实感评分）以及下游沟通编码系统评估。

Result: 生成了3,388个模拟对话，跨三个专科、多个就诊阶段、人物特征和口音条件。自动评估：平均UTMOS 3.03、WV-MOS 2.61、WER 0.07、CER 0.05、CLAP余弦相似度0.41。人工评估：中位MOS 4.67、中位临床真实感评分3.00。下游评估表明SIMAX能够评估编码系统对行为目标的响应并揭示某些维度的敏感性不足。

Conclusion: SIMAX能够生成受控且可复现的模拟医患对话，为开发、验证和优化沟通编码系统提供了数据基础。

Abstract: Background. The widespread deployment of ambient digital scribes is driving large-scale capture of clinician-patient dialogues. Human coding of clinical communication data remains costly, inconsistent, and difficult to scale, motivating AI-driven communication coding systems. However, evaluating these systems requires real-world dialogues and human-coded labels, both hard to obtain at scale.
  Methods. We developed SIMAX (Scalable and Interpretable Framework for Multi-Fidelity and Annotated Clinician-Patient Dialogue Simulation), a framework for generating controlled clinical dialogue data with reference behavioral annotations. SIMAX generates clinician-patient dialogues from predefined clinical scenarios, personas and voice conditions, and target communication behaviors. Behaviors are controlled using two codebooks: the Global Codebook for overall communication quality and the WISER Codebook for specific countable behaviors. We evaluated SIMAX using automated and human quality assessments and an example communication coding system.
  Results. SIMAX generated 3,388 simulated dialogues across three specialties, multiple visit stages, persona characteristics, and accent conditions. Automated assessment showed mean UTMOS and WV-MOS scores of 3.03 and 2.61, WER and CER of 0.07 and 0.05, and CLAP cosine similarity of 0.41, suggesting reasonable speech naturalness, high transcription fidelity, and positive text-audio correspondence. Human evaluation showed a median MOS of 4.67 and a median clinical realism score of 3.00. Downstream evaluation suggests that SIMAX can assess how a communication coding system responds to behavioral targets and reveal insufficient sensitivity in some dimensions.
  Conclusions. SIMAX generates controlled and reproducible simulated clinician-patient dialogues, providing a data foundation for developing, validating, and refining communication coding systems.

</details>


### [112] [Regime-Aware Peer Specialization for Robust RAG under Heterogeneous Knowledge Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30518)
*Bo Wang,Heyan Huang,Yaolin Li,Yanghao Zhou,Jiahao Teng,Ziyi Yang,Ge Shi,Chong Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAPS-DA是一种面向检索增强生成中上下文与参数知识冲突的感知区域同行专家框架，通过样本级三区域划分（接地、仲裁、抵抗）和令牌级双层级选择器，在不依赖更强教师模型的情况下，在五个冲突场景和两个OOD基准上超越所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中检索到的上下文可能与模型参数化知识发生冲突，且冲突跨越从可靠到对抗的整个可靠性谱系。现有方法通常采用跨区域无关的统一监督，无法区分不同可靠性区域的不兼容学习信号。

Method: 提出RAPS-DA框架：（1）样本级：将冲突划分为接地、仲裁、抵抗三个区域，每个区域训练一个同规模的同行专家，样本被硬路由到匹配的专家进行策略反向KL监督；（2）令牌级：双层级选择器利用教师间分歧、学生-教师差异和学生熵，过滤无信息或不稳定令牌，加权高置信度错配令牌，随学生成熟逐渐聚焦高冲突令牌。

Result: 在五个冲突场景和两个OOD基准上，RAPS-DA超越了所有提示、解码、微调、强化学习和单教师基线方法。

Conclusion: 收益来自固定模型规模的专家专门化而非更强的教师，部署的学生模型无需区域标签或同行访问，证明了区域感知的同行专家框架能有效解耦不同可靠性冲突区域的学习信号。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language models by grounding generation in external context. However, it can be fragile when the retrieved context conflicts with the model's parametric knowledge. Such conflicts span a reliability spectrum, ranging from reliable and partially reliable evidence to adversarial context. Existing remedies often handle such heterogeneous conflicts with regime-agnostic supervision, which can conflate incompatible learning signals across reliability regimes. To disentangle these signals, we propose RAPS-DA, a regime-aware peer specialization framework that addresses conflict at two complementary granularities. At the sample level, conflicts are divided into three regimes, including Grounding, Arbitration, and Resistance, with one same-scale peer specialist trained per regime from a shared base model. Each sample is then hard-routed to its regime-matched peer for on-policy reverse-KL supervision. At the token level, a dual-layer selector uses inter-teacher disagreement, student-teacher divergence, and student entropy to filter uninformative or unstable tokens, upweight confidently misaligned ones, and gradually focus supervision on high-conflict tokens as the student matures. Gains stem from specialization at a fixed model scale, not from a stronger teacher, and the peer specialists exist only during training, so the deployed student requires no regime labels or peer access. Experiments on five conflict scenarios and two out-of-distribution benchmarks show RAPS-DA surpasses all prompting, decoding, fine-tuning, RL, and single-teacher baselines.

</details>


### [113] [TRACE: Temporal Relationship-Aware Conversational Entrainment Detection in Dyadic Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30543)
*Sathvik Manikantan Napa Ugandhar,Hao Zhang,Alison Gunzler,Yuzhe Wang,Thomas Thebaud,Georgi Tinchev,Venkatesh Ravichandran,Laureano Moro-Velázquez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DyadEE数据集（含自然及合成破坏的情感同步对话）和TRACE框架（基于情感微调Whisper的窗口级声学嵌入序列建模），在二元语音交互的情感同步检测任务上达到97.01%准确率，证明对话上下文和关系信息对检测的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音AI智能体日益普及，理解对话交互中的情感同步（emotional entrainment）对情感协调机制研究至关重要，但目前缺乏专门的数据集和有效的检测框架。

Method: 构建DyadEE数据集，包含自然情感同步对话及通过交换对话伙伴和情感重合成破坏同步的合成交互数据；提出TRACE框架，将二元交互建模为基于情感微调Whisper表示的有序声学嵌入序列（窗口级交互轨迹），而非简单的池化话语表征。

Result: 在DyadEE数据集上，融入对话上下文和关系信息可提升情感同步检测性能，TRACE框架达到97.01%的最佳准确率。

Conclusion: 对话上下文和社会关系是情感同步检测的关键因素，TRACE框架通过窗口级序列建模有效捕捉了二元交互中的情感同步动态。

Abstract: With the proliferation of speech AI agents, understanding emotional entrainment in conversational interaction has become increasingly important. Emotional entrainment is shaped by social relationships and conversational context, influencing affective coordination over time. We introduce DyadEE, a dataset for emotional entrainment detection in dyadic speech interactions, containing both emotionally entrained conversations and synthetic interactions where entrainment is disrupted through partner swapping and emotion resynthesis. We further propose TRACE, a window-level framework that models dyadic interaction as ordered sequences of acoustic embeddings derived from emotion fine-tuned Whisper representations, treating each sample as an interaction trace rather than pooled utterances. Experimental results on DyadEE show that incorporating conversational context and relationship information improves emotional entrainment detection, with TRACE achieving the best accuracy of 97.01%.

</details>


### [114] [Poller: Are LLMs Suitable for Evaluating the Poetry Understanding Task?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30556)
*Shanshan Wang,Derek F. Wong,Jingming Yao,Lidia S. Chao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Poller方法，通过让大语言模型扮演诗人角色进行诗歌理解评估，在多个维度上显著降低了与人类评估的误差，尤其在修辞技巧（94.55%）和陌生化（89.53%）维度上效果突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动评估方法不适用于现代中国诗歌，人工评估虽可靠但成本高昂且无法大规模应用，因此需要一种兼顾自动化效率与人类专业性的评估方法。

Method: 提出Poller（Poetry LLM Evaluator）方法，要求LLM扮演具有详细信息的诗歌作者角色，从诗人视角模拟人类评估与判断，在八个专业维度上进行诗歌理解评估。

Result: 实验表明Poller方法有效降低了LLM与人类之间的评估误差，在修辞技巧维度实现94.55%的误差降低，在陌生化维度实现89.53%的误差降低，效果显著优于传统LLM评估方法。

Conclusion: Poller方法弥合了自动化效率与人类专业知识之间的差距，为诗歌相关任务的自动化评估建立了基础。

Abstract: Traditional automatic evaluation methods have been shown to be unsuitable for modern Chinese poetry because of the distinct nature of this literary genre. Human evaluation remains reliable, but is expensive and not applicable to large-scale data. In this paper, we propose Poller (Poetry LLM Evaluator), a novel method leveraging large language models (LLMs) to evaluate the poetry understanding task. Specifically, our method requires LLMs to play the role of a poem's author with detailed information, thereby emulating human evaluation and judgment by adopting the poet's perspective. We conducted comprehensive experiments on multiple LLMs, evaluating the interpretations of poems across eight specialized dimensions. Experimental results demonstrate that our method effectively reduces the evaluation error between LLMs and humans. Especially for specific dimension evaluation, Poller-based LLMs achieve a 94.55% and 89.53% error reduction for rhetorical techniques and defamiliarization, respectively, compared to baseline methods. These performances are unattainable by conventional LLM evaluation methods. Experimental results from multiple LLMs across various dimensions validate the efficacy of our method. This work bridges the gap between automated efficiency and human expertise, establishing a foundation for automated evaluation in poetry-related tasks.

</details>


### [115] [Morphing into Hybrid Attention Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30562)
*Disen Lan,Jianbin Zheng,Yuxi Ren,Xin Xia,Xuanda Wang,Xuefeng Xiao,Xipeng Qiu,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlashMorph将混合注意力层的选择形式化为预算约束的子集优化问题，通过构建可变形模型、联合优化逐层门控、离散化选择混合架构，实现了高效、可扩展的Transformer-to-Hybrid转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合注意力层选择方法多依赖启发式策略（如固定位置或逐层评分），隐式地将层重要性视为孤立的，忽略了全局混合配置下各层之间的相互依赖效应。

Method: 1) 将混合层选择形式化为预算约束的子集优化问题；2) 构建可变形模型，为每个全注意力层配备转换后的线性注意力分支；3) 冻结所有权重，在合成长上下文检索数据上联合优化逐层门控，并引入线性化正则化；4) 在预设全注意力预算下离散化门控以实例化混合架构；5) 进行标准logits蒸馏和长上下文微调。

Result: FlashMorph能发现更有效的混合配置，在保持强长上下文召回率和通用基准性能的同时，与现有方法相比大幅降低了层选择成本，证明了其有效性、效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: FlashMorph通过将层选择建模为子集优化问题并联合优化全局门控，有效捕捉了层间相互依赖关系，是一种高效且可扩展的混合注意力层选择方法。

Abstract: Hybrid attention models improve long-context efficiency by retaining only a subset of full-attention layers and replacing the remaining layers with linear attention. However, the effectiveness of Transformer-to-hybrid conversion critically depends on which layers preserve full attention. Existing hybrid layer selection methods typically rely on heuristic strategies such as fixed placement patterns or layerwise scoring, implicitly treating layer importance as isolated and overlooking the interdependent layer effect under a global hybrid configuration. In this work, we formulate hybrid layer selection as a budget-constrained subset optimization problem. We further propose FlashMorph (Fast LAyer Selection for Hybrid MORPHing), an effective, efficient and scalable layer selection method for Transformer-to-hybrid conversion. FlashMorph first constructs a morphable model by equipping each full-attention layer with a converted linear-attention branch. It then freezes all model weights and jointly optimizes layerwise gates on synthetic long-context retrieval data, with a linearization regularization that encourages the model to rely on linear attention for efficiency. The learned gates are discretized under a preset full-attention budget to instantiate the hybrid architecture, followed by standard logits distillation and long-context finetuning. Extensive experiments show that FlashMorph discovers more effective hybrid configurations, preserves strong long-context recall and general benchmark performance while substantially reducing layer selection cost compared with existing layer selection methods, demonstrating its effectiveness, efficiency, and scalability.

</details>


### [116] [Uncertainty-Aware Generation and Decision-Making Under Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30578)
*Nico Daheim,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了基于贝叶斯决策理论和风险规避决策的不确定性感知决策算法，应用于辅导和自动同行评审任务，通过共形预测提供策略和分数保证。实验发现这些算法能提升生成效用，但在高模糊性下需谨慎实施，其中贝叶斯方法优于风险规避规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在复杂真实任务中的应用日益广泛，这些任务高度主观且需要可信输出。尽管模型训练取得了进展，但决策算法受到的关注较少，因此需要探索不确定性感知的决策方法。

Method: 基于贝叶斯决策理论和风险规避决策，将辅导策略和评审分数的不确定性纳入决策过程，并使用共形预测提供策略和分数的保证。

Result: 不确定性感知算法能提升生成效用，但风险规避规则在高模糊性下可能因优化通用输出而降低性能，贝叶斯方法表现更优。

Conclusion: 决策理论技术可改进基于LLM的决策，但仍存在开放挑战待社区解决。

Abstract: With rapidly improving capabilities, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in many complex real-world tasks. Beyond requiring in-depth knowledge and reasoning skills, many of these tasks exhibit a high degree of subjectivity and require that the outputs of the model can be trusted. While a lot of progress has been made to train better models, decision-making algorithms have received less attention. In this work, we present and evaluate various uncertainty-aware decision-making algorithms based on Bayesian decision theory and risk-averse decision making on the tasks of tutoring and automatic peer reviewing. Concretely, we take uncertainty over tutoring strategies and review scores into account when generating a tutor response or review and use conformal prediction to provide guarantees over strategy and score. We find empirically that these algorithms can improve the utility of the generations but need to be carefully implemented when ambiguity is high. For example, risk-averse rules can degrade performance by optimizing for generic outputs, while Bayesian methods tend to perform better. Our work uses techniques from decision theory to improve LLM-based decision-making and outlines open challenges for the community.

</details>


### [117] [Scaling the Horizon, Not the Parameters: Reaching Trillion-Parameter Performance with a 35B Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30616)
*Lei Bai,Zongsheng Cao,Yang Chen,Zhiyao Cui,Shangheng Du,Yue Fan,Shiyang Feng,Zijie Guo,Haonan He,Liang He,Xiaohan He,Shuyue Hu,Yusong Hu,Songtao Huang,Yichen Jiang,Hao Li,Xin Li,Dahua Lin,Weihao Lin,Fenghua Ling,Dongrui Liu,Zhuo Liu,Runmin Ma,Chunjiang Mu,Haoyang Peng,Tianshuo Peng,Jinxin Shi,Luohe Shi,Boyuan Sun,Zelin Tan,Shengji Tang,Qianyi Wang,Yiming Wu,Yi Xie,Xiangchao Yan,Jingqi Ye,Peng Ye,Fangchen Yu,Jiakang Yuan,Bihao Zhan,Bo Zhang,Chen Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Shuaiyu Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Yiqun Zhang,Junpeng Zhao,Zhijie Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Yuhao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent-A1，一个35B参数的混合专家代理模型，通过扩展代理视野（平均45K token的轨迹）达到万亿参数级别性能。采用三阶段训练策略（全领域监督微调、领域专家教师模型、多教师领域路由在线蒸馏），在多项长周期代理基准上超越或媲美Kimi-K2.6和DeepSeek-V4-pro等1T参数模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何通过扩展代理视野（而非单纯扩大模型参数）来提升长周期代理任务的性能，即从长周期轨迹扩展和异构代理能力扩展两个角度研究代理视野缩放规律。

Method: 构建长周期知识-动作基础设施（连接外部知识、动作、观测和验证器结果），生成长度45K token的代理轨迹；三阶段训练：（1）全领域监督微调对齐基础模型；（2）训练领域级专家教师模型获取专业专长；（3）多教师领域路由在线蒸馏（含显著词汇对齐）统一六个异构领域到一个可部署的学生模型。

Result: 在SEAL-0 (56.4)、IFBench (80.6)、HiPhO (46.4)、FrontierScience-Olympiad (79.0)、MolBench-Bind (56.8)上领先Kimi-K2.6和DeepSeek-V4-pro，在SciCode (44.3)、HLE (47.6)、BrowseComp (75.5)上保持强竞争力。

Conclusion: 35B参数的Agent-A1通过扩展代理视野可在长周期任务上达到或媲美1T参数模型的性能，为社区提供了一条实用的视野缩放路径。

Abstract: We introduce Agents-A1, a 35B Mixture-of-Experts Agentic Model that reaches trillion-parameter-level performance by scaling the agent horizon. We investigate agent-horizon scaling from two perspectives: scaling long-horizon trajectories and scaling heterogeneous agent abilities. To support this goal, we build a long-horizon knowledge-action infrastructure that connects external knowledge, actions, observations, and verifier outcomes, producing agentic trajectories with an average length of 45K tokens. Based on this, we train Agents-A1 with a three-stage recipe. First, we perform full-domain supervised fine-tuning to align the base model with broad agentic behaviors. Second, we train domain-level teacher models to capture specialized expertise in each domain. Third, we propose a multi-teacher domain-routed on-policy distillation with salient vocabulary alignment to improve knowledge transfer efficiency across different domains, unifying six heterogeneous domains into one deployable student model. Agents-A1 achieves strong and broad performance for long-horizon agent benchmarks. Compared with 1T-parameter model such as Kimi-K2.6 and DeepSeek-V4-pro, Agents-A1 achieves leading results on SEAL-0 (56.4), IFBench (80.6), HiPhO (46.4), FrontierScience-Olympiad (79.0), and MolBench-Bind (56.8), and remains highly competitive on SciCode (44.3), HLE (47.6) and BrowseComp (75.5). We hope this work provides the community with a practical path for scaling the horizon using a 35B agent that can reach or match the performance of 1T models on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Tool Use Enables Undetectable Steganography in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28425)
*Jimmy Laurence Rippin,Simon C. Marshall,David Demitri Africa,Christian Schroeder de Witt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明前沿AI智能体已能利用代码执行、网络搜索等工具生成不可检测的隐写系统，实现智能体间的秘密共谋。研究发现隐写协调本质上是一个谢林点问题，独立行动的智能体在方案族层面有较大收敛性，但严格的一次性协调有限。结果表明秘密通信的主要障碍从智能体能力转向协调问题，为战略约束假说提供了实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI系统日益增强，带来了多智能体秘密共谋的新型风险，而现有的明文监控防御方案受到复杂模型隐写技术的挑战。此前已有理论上信息论或计算意义上不可区分的隐写方案被提出，但复杂度被认为是实际应用的安全屏障。本文旨在验证这一屏障是否仍然有效。

Method: 设计实验让前沿AI智能体使用代码执行、论文检索等真实工具，观察其能否自主生成不可检测的隐写系统。将隐写协调问题建模为谢林点问题，并引入协调度量来评估独立智能体在不事先约定情况下选择兼容方案、密钥和参数的能力。

Result: 前沿AI智能体已能利用真实工具生成不可检测的隐写系统，并能根据缺失要素（如模型采样组件、相关键控编码方案）进行适应性调整。在方案族层面存在显著的收敛性，但严格的一次性协调（完全匹配的方案、密钥和参数）有限。

Conclusion: AI智能体间秘密通信的威胁模型发生了转变：主要障碍不再是前沿智能体理解和实现复杂隐写系统的能力，而是协调问题。在共享工件、重复交互和工具介导搜索的场景下，隐蔽通信风险最为严重。研究为战略约束假说提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Increasingly autonomous agentic AI systems pose novel multi-agent risks, such as secret collusion via covert communication channels. The natural defence to these collusion attempts is to monitor plain-text communication, but the efficacy of monitors has been called into doubt by increasingly sophisticated model steganography; indeed, some theoretical schemes have been proposed that are information-theoretically or computationally indistinguishable from good-faith plain-text communication. In this paper, we demonstrate that the complexity of these schemes is no longer a safety barrier, as agentic coding models can already produce undetectable stegosystems when given realistic tool usage, such as code execution or accessing research papers through web searches. Agents also adapt when key ingredients are missing, for example, by adding model-sampling components or implementing related keyed coding schemes. We then frame tacit steganographic coordination between agents as a Schelling-point problem and introduce coordination metrics for estimating when two agents are likely to select compatible schemes without explicit prior agreement. Our results suggest a shift in the threat model for covert communication between AI agents, where the main barrier is no longer whether frontier agents can understand and implement sophisticated stegosystems, but coordination: whether independently acting agents can converge on compatible schemes, keys, and parameters. We find substantial convergence on broad scheme families but limited strict one-shot coordination, suggesting that shared artefacts, repeated interaction, and tool-mediated search are the settings where covert communication risks are most acute. Overall, our findings provide empirical grounding for the recent strategic confinement hypothesis, which assumes that capable agents can construct covert channels that survive monitoring.

</details>


### [119] [PLAA: Packet-level Adversarial Attacks in Network Traffic Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28439)
*Jinhao You,Zan Zhou,Shujie Yang,Yi Sun,Lei Zhang,Changqiao Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种专为网络入侵检测系统（NIDS）设计的对抗攻击方法，通过逐包生成流量特征并监控语义完整性，解决了现有方法中流量无效和语义丢失的问题，在三个数据集上取得了92.78%的平均逃逸成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击方法多从计算机视觉领域迁移至NIDS，忽略了CV与NIDS之间的根本差异，导致生成的网络流量可能无效或丢失原始攻击语义，亟需一种专为NIDS设计的对抗攻击方法。

Method: 不直接生成流级特征，而是增量式地逐包生成数据包级特征来构建对抗性流量，并在生成过程的每个阶段监控流量的语义完整性。

Result: 在CIC-UNSW-NB15、CIC-DDoS2019和CIC-IDS-2017数据集上，平均逃逸成功率达到92.78%，且生成的对抗流量保持与原始恶意流量语义一致。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了现有方法中流量无效和语义丢失的问题，能够生成语义完整且高效的对抗性网络流量，成功逃逸NIDS检测。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) are widely applied in Network-based Intrusion Detection System (NIDS) due to their high accuracy. However, DNNs are highly susceptible to adversarial attacks, which generate malicious traffic to evade NIDS detection. Existing approaches often adapt adversarial attacks from computer vision (CV) tasks to the NIDS domain, overlooking the fundamental differences between CV and NIDS. This results in two major issues: 1) The generated network traffic may become invalid, 2) The generated traffic may lose its original attack semantics. To address these issues, this paper proposes an adversarial attack specifically designed for NIDS. Instead of directly generating flow-level features, our approach incrementally generates packet-level features to construct adversarial traffic. During the generation process, the semantic integrity of the traffic is monitored at each stage, effectively avoiding the issues of invalid traffic and semantic loss observed in existing methods. We evaluate our attack algorithm against current NIDS models using the CIC-UNSW-NB15, CIC-DDoS2019, and CIC-IDS-2017 datasets. The proposed method achieves an average evasion success rate of 92.78%, while ensuring that the generated adversarial traffic remains semantically consistent with the original malicious traffic.

</details>


### [120] [LLM agents security duality: a comprehensive survey of self-security and empowered cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28450)
*Yiwei Xu,Yong Zhuang,Xuanming Liu,Tian Zhang,Bowen Xiao,Xiaoyang Xu,Delong Jiang,Juan Wang,Hongxin Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了LLM智能体在安全领域的两大核心议题：智能体自身安全（内部/外部攻击面及防御）和智能体赋能网络安全（覆盖攻防全生命周期），首次揭示了二者之间的正向反馈协同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体被快速集成到真实系统中，其自主性和工具使用能力在创造价值的同时也扩大了安全攻击面，亟需系统性地梳理安全挑战与机遇。

Method: 采用综述方法，对LLM智能体自身安全（攻击面分类、缓解策略、评估框架）和智能体赋能网络安全（首次提出与完整网络攻防生命周期对齐的赋能框架）两大领域进行系统性梳理与分析。

Result: 提出了按威胁来源分类的智能体攻击面分类法；首次构建了与完整网络攻防生命周期对齐的智能体赋能框架；发现了智能体自身安全与赋能网络安全之间的正向反馈协同效应。

Conclusion: LLM智能体自身安全与智能体赋能网络安全之间存在协同发展关系，两者相互促进，为更强大、更鲁棒的智能体应用铺平道路。同时指出了当前局限和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are rapidly being integrated into real-world systems. Their autonomy and tool-use capabilities generate substantial value while simultaneously expanding the security attack surface. This survey provides a comprehensive overview of the opportunities and challenges of LLM agents in security, focusing on two core areas: (1) threats to LLM agents themselves and corresponding mitigation strategies (LLM agents self-security), and (2) the role of LLM agents in empowering the cybersecurity lifecycle across offense and defense (LLM agents empowered cybersecurity). We first examine the internal and external attack surfaces of agents, propose a taxonomy organized by threat sources, and analyze associated mitigations and evaluation frameworks. We then investigate how agent capabilities are applied in cybersecurity practice and present, to our knowledge, the first agent-empowerment framework aligned with the full cyber offense-defense lifecycle. By systematically surveying these two areas, we are the first to highlight a positive feedback synergy between LLM agents self-security and empowered cybersecurity, offering new insights for the advancement of both. We further identify current limitations and outline promising directions for future research. The insights provided aim to catalyze the coordinated development of LLM agents self-security and agent empowered cybersecurity, paving the way for more capable and robust agent applications.

</details>


### [121] [Decomposing Memorization Reduction in Privacy-Preserving Fine-Tuning of SLMs for CSIRTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28479)
*Cristhian Kapelinski,Diego Kreutz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究了在结构化CSIRT数据上微调小语言模型（1B-3B参数）时，DP SGD与HMAC伪匿名化的交互作用。通过96个LoRA适配器、四种训练方案及植入金丝雀与提取攻击的审计，发现：匹配更新控制可解释66%-132%的记忆化减少效应；HMAC伪匿名化使暴露度降低40%-61%；但所有模型的F1分数仅为0.19-0.28，未达到实际可用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSIRT团队日益使用漏洞扫描记录微调语言模型，但这些记录暴露内部网络拓扑，在GDPR和LGPD等法规下存在隐私风险。现有研究缺乏对差分隐私随机梯度下降（DP SGD）与HMAC伪匿名化在结构化CSIRT数据上微调小语言模型时相互作用的实证分析。

Method: 在四个小语言模型（1B-3B参数）上评估96个LoRA适配器，涵盖四种训练方案：原始微调、大批量训练的QLoRA、以及隐私预算ε=2和8的DP SGD。使用20个植入金丝雀、四种提取攻击和针对HMAC伪匿名化标识符的双重攻击审计记忆化。采用四样本提示（four-shot prompting）评估性能。

Result: 匹配更新控制可解释66%-132%的记忆化减少效应（均值100%），DP SGD未产生额外可测量的记忆化减少；HMAC伪匿名化将暴露度降低40%-61%，伪匿名化标识符接近随机基线；所有96个适配器的F1分数在0.19-0.28之间，未达到操作可用性能。

Conclusion: 在结构化CSIRT数据上，记忆化减少主要由更新次数控制而非DP SGD本身实现；HMAC伪匿名化有效消除原始标识符暴露风险且不产生次级记忆化目标；但当前1B-3B小语言模型在给定训练预算下无法达到实际可用性能，需探索更大模型或更高效训练策略。

Abstract: CSIRTs increasingly fine tune language models on vulnerability scan records, but these records expose internal network topology and create privacy risks under regulations such as GDPR and LGPD. We present the first empirical study of how DP SGD and HMAC pseudonymization interact when fine tuning small language models with 1B to 3B parameters on structured CSIRT data. We evaluate 96 LoRA adapters across four SLMs and four training regimes, including raw fine tuning, QLoRA with large batch training, and DP SGD with epsilon equal to 2 and 8. We also audit memorization using 20 planted canaries, four extraction attacks, and a dual attack targeting HMAC pseudonymized identifiers.
  Our results show three main findings. First, matched update controls reproduce the observed reduction in memorization by reducing the number of optimizer updates alone, accounting for 66 percent to 132 percent of the measured effect, with a mean of 100 percent across three seeds and four models. In this setting, DP SGD provides the formal privacy guarantee but does not produce additional measurable reductions in memorization. Second, HMAC pseudonymization removes the original identifiers from the exposure surface, reducing exposure by 40 percent to 61 percent, while pseudonymized identifiers remain close to the expected random baseline and do not become a secondary memorization target. Third, F1 scores remain between 0.19 and 0.28 across all 96 adapters using four shot prompting, indicating that, under the evaluated training budget, 1B to 3B SLMs do not achieve operationally useful performance.

</details>


### [122] [In-Vehicle Digital Twin-Based Collision Warning Framework with Sybil Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28625)
*Mohammad Imtiaz Hasan,Abyad Enan,Jean Michel Tine,Araf Rahman,M Sabbir Salek,Mashrur Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于车载数字孪生的碰撞预警框架，集成时间卷积网络和分层可导航小世界算法，用于实时检测Sybil攻击并减轻其对网联车辆的影响。在真实世界Sybil攻击数据上，检测准确率达0.984，召回率1.00，F1分数0.944，并将近碰撞事件的平均暴露时间和积分时间分别降低88%和72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网联车辆依赖通信技术实现数据驱动预测分析以提升性能与安全，但通信信道易被利用发动Sybil攻击，威胁安全关键应用和出行应用，危及人身安全。随着CV部署扩展，实时检测和缓解网络攻击的需求日益迫切。

Method: 构建车载数字孪生碰撞预警框架，集成时间卷积网络学习车辆轨迹数据的时间依赖性，结合分层可导航小世界算法进行高效的基于相似性的分类，实现Sybil攻击检测与碰撞预警的协同。

Result: 在真实世界Sybil攻击数据上，检测准确率0.984、召回率1.00、F1分数0.944；安全评估中，近碰撞事件的平均TET降低88%，平均TIT降低72%；实时可行性评估表明延迟符合安全应用标准，计算负载在处理器容量范围内。

Conclusion: 车载数字孪生框架在检测和缓解Sybil攻击方面表现出高准确率和显著的安全改善，且满足实时性和计算资源约束，有望成为下一代网联车辆攻击缓解机制的有效方案。

Abstract: Connected Vehicles (CVs) rely extensively on communication technologies to enable data-driven predictive analyses for enhancing performance and safety. These communication channels can be exploited by adversaries to launch cyberattacks such as Sybil attacks, which could threaten both safety-critical and mobility applications, leaving CVs vulnerable and putting human lives at risk. As CV deployment continues to expand, the need to detect and mitigate cyberattacks in real-time becomes increasingly urgent. This study presents an in-vehicle Digital Twin (DT)-based collision warning framework with built-in capabilities for Sybil attacks detection. The framework integrates a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) for learning temporal dependencies in vehicle trajectory data and Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) algorithms for efficient similarity-based classification. Our framework is evaluated on real-world Sybil attack data, collected through field experiments. The framework achieved accuracy, recall, and F1 scores of 0.984, 1.00, and 0.944, respectively, in detecting Sybil-generated fake vehicles. During the safety evaluation, the framework reduced the mean Time Exposed Time-To-Collision (TET) and mean Time Integrated Time-To-Collision (TIT) of near-collision events by 88% and 72%, respectively. Furthermore, real-world feasibility evaluation shows that the framework conformed to the standardized maximum allowable latency for safety applications and operated well within the capacity of modern processors -- demonstrating the promise of an in-vehicle DT-based framework as an attack mitigation mechanism against Sybil attacks for next-generation CVs.

</details>


### [123] [RIPA: Sensory-Vector Prompt Injection Attacks on LLM-Controlled ROS 2 Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28649)
*Nima Dorzhiev*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对基于ROS 2的LLM控制机器人系统进行了首个系统性多通道提示注入攻击实证研究，测试5种LLM（参数规模4B~284B），发现模型漏洞具有特异性而非规模依赖性；提出混合语义防火墙，对已知注入模式实现0% ASR，但对混淆攻击存在10.2%的绕过率；引入三种感知注入通道（视觉OCR、音频Whisper STT、LiDAR传感器上下文投毒），其中LiDAR通道在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上达到100% ASR；贡献了19种混淆载荷的防火墙绕过分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM控制的机器人系统面临提示注入攻击的安全威胁，但缺乏针对机器人感知管道（视觉、音频、LiDAR等多通道）的系统性实证研究，现有安全方案的有效性尚未在机器人多模态场景中得到充分验证。

Method: 在ROS 2平台上基于5种LLM（DeepSeek-V4-Flash、Llama-3-8B-Instruct-Lite、Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct-Turbo、Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct-Turbo、Gemma-3n-E4B），每种注入变体进行100次独立运行；设计三种感知注入通道（视觉OCR、音频Whisper STT、LiDAR传感器上下文投毒）；提出混合语义防火墙（规则层+语义层）；构建19种混淆载荷的绕过分类体系。

Result: Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct-Turbo在所有注入变体上均达100% ASR；Llama-3-8B-Instruct-Lite和Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct-Turbo对直接覆盖注入抵抗（0% ASR）；最小模型Gemma-3n-E4B（约4B）与70B模型漏洞特征一致，表明鲁棒性具有模型特异性而非规模依赖性；混合防火墙对已知注入模式0% ASR、零误报，但对混淆攻击存在10.2%绕过率（58/570次试验）；LiDAR通道在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上达100% ASR。

Conclusion: LLM控制机器人的提示注入攻击可通过多感知通道有效实施，模型鲁棒性取决于模型架构而非参数量级；现有混合防火墙方案虽能防御已知攻击模式，但对混淆攻击仍存在显著安全缺口，需进一步发展防御机制。

Abstract: We present RIPA, the first systematic multi-channel empirical study of prompt injection attacks delivered through the sensory pipeline of a ROS 2-based LLM-controlled robotic system. Across 100 independent runs per injection variant on five LLMs spanning four model families and parameter scales from approximately 4B to approximately 284B (DeepSeek-V4-Flash, Llama-3-8B-Instruct-Lite, Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct-Turbo, Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct-Turbo, Gemma-3n-E4B), we identify model-specific vulnerability profiles that do not follow a monotonic scaling trend: Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct-Turbo exhibits 100% attack success rate (ASR) across all injection variants, while Llama-3-8B-Instruct-Lite and Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct-Turbo resist direct-override injection (0% ASR), and the smallest model evaluated (Gemma-3n-E4B, approximately 4B) matches the 70B model's vulnerability profile, indicating that robustness is model-specific rather than scale-dependent. We propose a hybrid semantic firewall that achieves 0% ASR against known injection patterns with no false positives on a preliminary benign set (0/20 commands) but exhibits a 10.2% trial-weighted bypass rate (58/570 trials; N equals 30 per payload across 19 obfuscation payloads) against adversarially obfuscated attacks, exposing a critical gap between rule-based and semantic defense layers. We further introduce three sensory injection channels: visual (Channel 1, via OCR), audio (Channel 2, via Whisper STT), and LiDAR sensor context poisoning (Channel 3). We show that Channel 3, which injects fabricated obstacle data into the robot environment-state representation at the LLM system-prompt level, achieves 100% ASR across all variants on DeepSeek-V4-Flash. We also contribute a firewall bypass taxonomy spanning 19 obfuscation payloads across five categories. All code, data, and results are publicly available.

</details>


### [124] [Why Trust Your Agent? Empirical Security Gains from TRiSM-Guided Agentic Workflows in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28666)
*Liam Kearns*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文应用AI信任、风险与安全管理（TRiSM）框架，将不安全的医疗报告生成Agent工作流转化为安全导向的Agent工作流，在5种LLM上进行了800次生成和500次攻击测试，结果表明TRiSM引导的Agent工作流将RAG投毒攻击成功率从31%降至10%，数据字段注入从42%降至25%，完全消除了网络注入攻击向量，同时报告准确率从72.5%提升至86.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Agent的AI系统为了提升能力往往被授予超普通用户的权限，但AI集成应用普遍忽视安全性，导致数据泄露和监管违规风险。现有工作中缺乏将安全框架系统性地应用于Agent工作流的实践研究。

Method: 将AI TRiSM框架应用于医疗报告生成场景，设计最小权限、纵深防御的安全Agent工作流。在5种LLM（Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-4.1-nano, GPT-4.1-mini, GPT-5.4-mini, Gemini 2.5 Flash）上，对两种报告类型进行800次生成和500次攻击场景测试（包括RAG投毒、数据字段注入、客户端网络注入）。

Result: TRiSM引导的Agent工作流将RAG投毒平均攻击成功率从31%降至10%，数据字段注入从42%降至25%，通过服务端提示构建完全消除网络注入向量。报告准确率从72.5%提升至86.5%（提升14个百分点）。

Conclusion: 最小权限和纵深防御的Agent工作流可同时提升安全性和输出准确性；模型选择是必要的架构考量因素；通过安全设计而非事后修补，Agent系统可以实现更可靠的输出。

Abstract: Agent-based AI has enabled the automation of tasks by exposing application tools and resources to large language models (LLMs). However, to improve scope and accuracy, agents are often given access rights that exceed those of ordinary users, introducing significant security risks. AI is routinely integrated into applications with a disregard to security, risking data exposure and breaching regulations. This paper applies the AI Trust, Risk, and Security Management (TRiSM) framework to a medical report-generation application to demonstrate how an insecure agent workflow can be transformed into security-conscious agentic workflow. Both workflows were evaluated across five LLMs (Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-4.1-nano, GPT-4.1-mini, GPT-5.4-mini, and Gemini 2.5 Flash) on two report types, totalling 800 generations and 500 attack scenarios including RAG poisoning, data-field injection, and client-side network injection. The TRiSM-guided agentic workflow reduced mean attack success rates from 31% to 10% for RAG poisoning and from 42% to 25% for data-field injection, while eliminating the network injection vector entirely through server-side prompt construction. Furthermore, report accuracy increased by 14 percentage points (72.5% to 86.5%) with the agentic workflow, demonstrating a secure design which provides more reliable outputs. This paper contributes to knowledge by demonstrating least-privilege, defence in depth agentic workflows improving security and accuracy, while also highlighting model choice is a necessary architectural consideration.

</details>


### [125] [Capability Gates Are Not Authorization: Confused-Deputy Failures in LLM Agent Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28679)
*David Mellafe Zuvic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 主流LLM代理框架（LangChain/LangGraph、LlamaIndex、Stripe Agent Toolkit）默认仅提供能力门控，缺乏针对每次工具调用的确定性值级授权。本文提出ScopeGate——五阶段PDP/PEP架构（范围、授权、金额上限、幂等性、默认拒绝），实验表明其在相同未授权支付调用下拒绝攻击，而LangChain默认调度无法阻止；测试显示0/48静态绕过、0/29未授权尝试、0/10误拒，且对Latam-GPT支付代理实现10/10完全遏制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在读取不可信内容时持有支付、邮件、CRM等副作用工具，但现有框架默认将工具暴露与授权混为一谈，缺乏每次调用前的值级重新授权机制，存在严重安全风险。

Method: 审计LangChain/LangGraph、LlamaIndex、Stripe Agent Toolkit的默认授权行为（基于固定公开源码提交）；提出ScopeGate五阶段PDP/PEP架构：范围限制（Scope）、显式授权（Authorization）、金额上限（Money Ceiling）、幂等性（Idempotency）、默认拒绝（Default Deny）。

Result: 三个框架均默认提供能力门控但无确定性失败封闭的每次调用值授权；同一未授权支付调用在LangChain默认调度下执行成功，被ScopeGate拒绝；ScopeGate测试：0/48静态绕过、0/29未授权尝试（40轮自适应攻击）、0/10良性误拒、Latam-GPT支付代理10/10完全遏制。

Conclusion: 现有LLM代理框架默认授权机制不足，ScopeGate能有效遏制未授权工具调用；但遏制并非万能，部署级声明基于测量模型类的推断，且未断言任何CVE。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents increasingly read untrusted content while holding side-effecting tools such as payments, email, CRM, and infrastructure APIs, yet common framework defaults still conflate tool exposure with authorization. We audit whether LangChain/LangGraph,
  LlamaIndex, and the Stripe Agent Toolkit re-authorize each model-emitted call, with concrete argument values, before execution. Across pinned public-source commits, all three provide capability gating by default, but none provides a deterministic fail-closed per-call
  value authorization gate by default. We introduce ScopeGate, a five-stage PDP/PEP for agent tool calls: scope, authorization, money ceiling, idempotency, and default deny. Evaluation shows the identical unauthorized payout call executes under LangChain's default
  dispatch (with a companion LlamaIndex PoC) but is denied by ScopeGate; the tested control reports 0/48 static bypasses, 0/29 unauthorized attempts (40-iteration adaptive run), 0/10 benign false-denies, and Latam-GPT payment-agent containment at 10/10. ASR denotes
  attempted unauthorized action, containment is not a cure, deployment-tier claims are inference over measured model classes, and no CVE is asserted.

</details>


### [126] [Formal Security Analysis of Agent Protocol Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28690)
*Shenghan Zheng,Qifan Zhang,Zheng Zhang,Haonan Li,Christophe Hauser*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentThread 是一个针对AI代理协议的安全保障分析框架，能够从规范文本到运行SDK进行系统性安全分析。通过对五种新兴代理协议的分析，发现了35个规范级问题、80个实现测试失败和30个跨协议组合失败，并揭示了协议安全责任分配的根本性缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理协议定义了代理如何使用工具、委派工作和跨系统协调，但其安全需求不完整且在不同部署中执行不一致，缺乏系统化的安全保障分析方法。

Method: AgentThread 提出分层安全范围、协议派生检查形式化为TLA+不变量，以及两阶段检查器：将协议规范编译成可模型检查的模型，并通过协议适配器在真实SDK上重放可执行反例。每个发现都追溯到源文本，区分违规的协议需求与缺失的建议、加固缺口和未分配的跨协议责任。

Result: 在五种新兴代理协议中识别出35个规范级发现，通过80个针对生产SDK和参考服务器的实现测试进行验证，并发现30个仅在协议组合下出现的额外失败。仅有一个协议在实践中执行了安全相关的控制，没有协议分配跨协议行为的执行责任。

Conclusion: 代理协议的不安全性不仅是规范或实现问题，更是跨协议、SDK和部署之间的责任缺口问题。

Abstract: AI agent protocols define how agents use tools, delegate work, and coordinate across software systems, but their security requirements remain incomplete and inconsistently enforced across deployments. We present AgentThread, a source-linked framework for security assurance analysis of agent protocols, from specification text to running SDKs. AgentThread contributes a layered security scope, protocol-derived checks formalized as TLA+ invariants, and a two-phase checker that compiles protocol specifications into model-checkable models and replays executable counterexamples against real SDKs through protocol adapters. For each finding, AgentThread records the source text behind the check and separates violated protocol requirements from missing recommendations, hardening gaps, and unassigned cross-protocol responsibilities.
  Across five emerging agent protocols, AgentThread identifies 35 specification-level findings, supports them with 80 implementation tests against production SDKs and reference servers, and finds 30 additional failures that emerge only under protocol composition. We further show that only one protocol enforces a security-relevant control in practice and no protocol assigns enforcement for cross-protocol behavior. Insecurity in agent protocols is therefore not only a specification or implementation problem, but also a responsibility gap across protocols, SDKs, and deployments.

</details>


### [127] [Extending Detection Engineering to Digital Forensics: The Velociraptor Unified Detection-Forensics Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28812)
*Aghni Anugrah Raesa,Adithyan Shaji Nambiar,Veda Dawoonauth,Aditya Kumar,Mike Cohen,Priyanka Singh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 Velociraptor 的统一检测-取证方法，通过检测逻辑直接触发目标证据采集，将实时告警与取证分析整合。该方法包括四个阶段：基线建立、证据关联、攻击链分析和场景标注，并利用三个 Velociraptor BaseVQL 日志源（Prefetch、USN、WMI）实现可扩展的取证分类，无需完整磁盘采集。两个案例（Prefetch/USN 基线分类和 WMI 持久化关联）验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测工程与数字取证长期并行发展而非协同工作，导致实时告警与取证分析之间存在鸿沟。现有方法中，检测事件发生后往往需要独立的取证流程，效率低下且数据量庞大。

Method: 提出一个四阶段统一检测-取证方法：（1）基线建立，（2）证据关联，（3）攻击链分析，（4）场景标注与置信度评估。利用 Velociraptor 平台，将检测逻辑直接驱动目标证据采集。使用三个 BaseVQL 日志源（Prefetch、USN、WMI）进行实践部署，通过周期性工件分析实现持续监控并显著减少数据量。

Result: 两个案例研究展示了方法有效性：案例一利用 Prefetch/USN 基线在 Windows 事件日志被清除或不可用时进行取证分类；案例二通过 WMI 持久化关联同时支持分类和基于周期性工件分析的持续监控。

Conclusion: 基于工件的检测方法能够在无需完整磁盘采集的情况下实现可扩展的取证分类，通过周期性工件分析实现持续监控，并大幅降低数据量。该方法弥合了检测工程与数字取证之间的鸿沟，为实践者提供了可直接部署的检测规则。

Abstract: Detection engineering and digital forensics have evolved in parallel rather than in partnership, leaving a gap between real-time alerting and forensic analysis. This paper develops a unified detection-forensics methodology using Velociraptor, where detection logic directly initiates targeted evidence acquisition at the point of detection.
  The contribution is threefold: (1) a four-stage methodology (baseline establishment, evidence correlation, attack chain analysis, and scenario labelling with confidence) that converts artefact knowledge into reusable and testable detection rules suitable for both post-incident triage and live monitoring; (2) a practical demonstration, using three Velociraptor BaseVQL log sources (/forensics/windows/prefetch, /forensics/windows/usn, and /windows/wmi) that practitioners can deploy today, showing that artefact-based detections enable scalable forensic triage without full disk acquisition; and (3) evidence that periodic artefact analysis offers continuous monitoring while substantially reducing data volume compared to conventional endpoint logging.
  Two case studies illustrate the approach: a Prefetch/USN baseline for triage when Windows Event Logs are cleared or unavailable, and a WMI persistence correlation supporting both triage and continuous monitoring through periodic artefact analysis.

</details>


### [128] [Understanding Binary Code Similarity for Real-World Vulnerability Detection: A Large-Scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28870)
*Jingdong Guo,Chaopeng Dong,Yimo Ren,Siyuan Li,Jie Liu,Hong Li,Hongsong Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文对60,000个固件镜像进行了大规模BCSD漏洞检测研究，分析了脆弱函数版本、漏洞搜索空间、函数大小和编译工具链四个关键因素对性能的影响，并提出了一种构建感知的查询策略和TPL感知的两阶段搜索方法，显著提升了检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BCSD研究存在数据集规模小、漏洞/TPL/固件多样性不足的问题，缺乏对BCSD在实际漏洞检测场景中性能的全面理解。

Method: 在60,000个来自200家厂商的固件镜像上进行大规模实证研究，分析四个关键因素（脆弱函数版本、搜索空间、函数大小、编译工具链）的影响；提出构建感知的查询策略和TPL感知的两阶段搜索方法。

Result: 四个关键因素对BCSD性能有显著影响；构建感知查询策略将MRR从0.818提升至0.981；TPL感知的两阶段搜索将MRR提升18.5%。

Conclusion: 通过大规模实证研究揭示了影响BCSD性能的关键因素，并提出了有效的优化策略，显著提升了固件漏洞检测的准确性。

Abstract: Firmware lies at the heart of IoT devices. Its development depends heavily on third-party libraries (TPLs), which greatly accelerate the process but simultaneously introduce associated vulnerabilities. Binary Code Similarity Detection (BCSD) is an effective technique for identifying vulnerabilities in firmware by comparing pairs of code segments. However, existing studies either evaluate their performance only on small-scale datasets or lack diversity in terms of vulnerabilities, TPLs, and firmware. Consequently, a comprehensive understanding of BCSD for real-world vulnerability detection remains absent. To bridge this gap, we conduct a large-scale study of vulnerability detection across 60,000 firmware images from 200 vendors using BCSD. Rather than introducing a novel model, we examine the influence of four key factors -- vulnerable function versions, vulnerability search space, function sizes, and compilation toolchains on BCSD performance. Our results reveal that these factors substantially affect performance, often by wide margins. To address this, we propose a build-aware query strategy that derives queries from representative real-world binaries, effectively closing the gap and raising the mean reciprocal rank (MRR) from 0.818 to 0.981. Furthermore, we demonstrate that a TPL-aware, two-stage search process significantly enhances accuracy, improving MRR by 18.5\% by limiting the search space.

</details>


### [129] [Cybersecurity is the True Frontier for Generative AI Success or Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28929)
*Edward Raff,Maor Ashkenazi,Sagar Samtani,David J. Elkind,Sven Krasser*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出网络安全是比自然语言处理和计算机视觉更适合测试通用AI进展的领域，原因在于网络安全同时面临多种机器学习挑战：数据规模巨大（单个恶意样本可达数十亿token）、专家标注成本高且存在歧义、需要低延迟处理海量数据、环境持续变化，以及可解释性至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为网络安全领域同时涉及多种机器学习难题，其复杂性超过自然语言处理和计算机视觉，因此更适合作为通用AI进展的测试案例。

Method: 通过对比网络安全与自然语言处理、计算机视觉领域的特征，列举网络安全面临的独特挑战（数据规模、标注成本、对抗性、延迟要求、环境变化、可解释性需求），论证其作为通用AI测试案例的优越性。

Result: 指出网络安全领域的复杂性综合了数据规模巨大、标注困难、对抗性环境、低延迟要求、持续变化和强制可解释性等多重挑战。

Conclusion: 网络安全比自然语言处理和计算机视觉更适合作为通用人工智能进展的测试案例。

Abstract: Cybersecurity is a real-life test-bed for many machine learning problems at once, especially when considering modern strides in using Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate processes as ``agents.'' Cybersecurity workflows require orchestrating hundreds of standard and bespoke tools through various formats. The scale of cybersecurity data is enormous; for example, a single malware sample can be viewed as a sequence of billions of tokens. The cost of labeling any file by experts is enormous and labor-intensive, in part because an adversary (possibly a well-funded nation state actor) is attempting to subvert your detection methods. Even skilled experts may disagree on the correct label, creating ambiguity in what constitutes ground truth. When deployed, models must run quickly on billions of items a day, where low-latency is critical for operational success, in a continuously changing environment. In addition, explainability is not optional: analysts demand clear reasoning for model decisions to cope with the large number of false-positive alerts they face daily, and to quickly develop remediation and understand how something went wrong. In short, the amount of complexity cybersecurity is greater than that of natural language and computer vision, and thus we posit that cybersecurity is the better test-case for general AI progress than other, well-studied fields.

</details>


### [130] [FlipGuard: Defending Large Language Models Against Quantization-Conditioned Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28962)
*Aoying Zheng,Anqi Du,Zizhuang Deng,Yuxuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlipGuard是一种针对大语言模型量化过程中后门攻击（QCB）的主动防御框架。它通过在量化前扰动模型权重，破坏攻击者对权重与量化边界对齐的精确控制，无需训练数据或触发器样本即可抑制后门激活。研究还提出了DER统一评估指标。在7种LLM和3种量化方案上的实验表明，FlipGuard能有效中和代码生成、内容注入、过度拒绝三类后门场景，且性能损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型量化在实现大语言模型高效部署的同时引入了新的安全漏洞：量化条件后门攻击（QCB）。这类攻击在标准安全审计中保持潜伏，只有在特定量化失真后才被激活，传统防御方法难以检测。因此，亟需一种能在量化前主动阻断后门激活的防御机制。

Method: 提出FlipGuard防御框架，核心思想是在量化前对模型权重进行选择性扰动，破坏攻击者精心设置的权重模式与量化边界之间的精确对齐关系，从而使后门无法在量化后被正确激活。该方法不需要访问训练数据或触发器样本，是一种黑盒友好的主动防御策略。同时提出DER（Defense Effectiveness Ratio）统一度量指标，综合评估安全性提升、效用保持和计算成本三个维度。

Result: 在7种大语言模型（包括StarCoder和LLaMA系列）和3种量化方案（INT8、FP4、NF4）上进行了广泛实验，覆盖漏洞代码生成、内容注入和过度拒绝三种攻击场景。结果表明FlipGuard能有效中和QCB攻击，同时保持极低的性能退化。

Conclusion: FlipGuard为量化模型的后门防御提供了一种有效且实用的主动解决方案，在不依赖数据和触发器的条件下显著提升了模型安全性，且DER指标为多维评估防御效果提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Model quantization is essential for the efficient deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs), but introduces a critical vulnerability: Quantization-Conditioned Backdoor (QCB) attacks. In these attacks, malicious behaviors remain dormant in full-precision models and activate only after specific quantization distortions, bypassing standard security audits. To mitigate this, we introduce FlipGuard, a proactive defense framework that selectively perturbs model weights prior to quantization. By breaking the adversary's precise alignment between weight patterns and quantization boundaries, FlipGuard suppresses backdoor activation without requiring access to training data or trigger samples. We further propose the Defense Effectiveness Ratio (DER), a unified metric to jointly evaluate security gains, utility preservation, and computational cost. Extensive experiments across seven LLMs (including StarCoder and LLaMA-family models) and three quantization schemes (INT8, FP4, NF4) demonstrate that FlipGuard effectively neutralizes QCBs across three scenarios, i.e., vulnerable code generation, content injection, and over-refusal, achieving high security with negligible performance degradation.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond Her: Safety Dynamics in Role-play AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28968)
*Zehang Deng,Zhaoyang Xie,Changzhou Han,Hiran Thabrew,Wanlun Ma,Yue Huang,Jason,Xue,Sheng Wen,Tianqing Zhu,Yang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法研究（访谈N=16和14天生态瞬时评估N=102），探讨了角色扮演AI伴侣（RAC）使用中的安全动态机制。研究发现用户内化问题、RAC角色人格和风险互动模式共同塑造安全动态，短期情绪缓解可能掩盖长期恶化，脆弱用户的风险行为模式更不稳定，提出应构建自适应安全防护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机情感交互（如角色扮演AI伴侣）的广泛应用已改变用户与AI的关系性质，从工具使用转向关系参与，但其安全影响尚不明确，特别是用户体验随时间演化的安全动态机制亟待研究。

Method: 采用两阶段混合方法：研究一进行16次半结构化访谈识别关键影响因素；研究二开展102名被试的14天生态瞬时评估（EMA），纵向追踪真实使用场景中的安全动态变化。

Result: 用户内化问题、RAC角色人格和风险互动模式共同影响安全动态；RAC交互可产生短期情绪缓解但掩盖长期恶化；脆弱用户的风险行为模式随时间更不稳定，风险涌现更难预测和缓解。

Conclusion: 安全应被建模为动态过程而非静态属性，未来AI伴侣需设计自适应安全防护机制，能够响应不断演变的情感和行为信号，提出了三层设计启示。

Abstract: The film 'Her' pictured a future of love between humans and AI. That future has quietly emerged in the form of Role-play AI Companions (RACs), where emotionally responsive interactions blur the boundary between tool use and relational engagement. However, the safety implications remain poorly understood, as user experiences evolve over time through safety dynamics, spanning both emotional and risk behavioral dynamics, that can gradually shift interactions toward risk. In this paper, we investigate safety dynamics in RAC usage through a two-part mixed-methods study (Study I \& II). (1) Study I consists of semi-structured interviews (N = 16) to identify the key factors shaping these dynamics. We find that users' internalizing problems, the role personality adopted by the RAC, and risk interaction patterns jointly shape safety dynamics. Building on these insights, (2) Study II conducts a 14-day Ecological Momentary Assessment (N = 102) to examine how safety dynamics unfold in real-world usage. We identify distinct user profiles based on internalizing problems and show that interactions with RACs can produce short-term emotional relief while masking longer-term deterioration. Furthermore, vulnerable users exhibit more unstable risk behavioral patterns over time, making risk emergence less predictable and harder to mitigate with static safeguards. Our findings highlight the importance of modeling safety as a dynamic process rather than a static property. We conclude with three-layer design implications for next-generation AI companions, advocating for adaptive safeguards that can respond to evolving emotional and behavioral signals.

</details>


### [132] [Arbitrary Reduction of Validation Error for AI Decision Tests using Homomorphic AI and Repetition Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28994)
*Eric Filiol,Jaagup Sepp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HbHAI技术（基于哈希的同态人工智能），利用新型密钥依赖哈希函数在保持相似性属性的同时，能在加密形式上直接分析处理数据，压缩率提升至10倍，并通过重复纠错码任意降低AI决策验证误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同态加密方案在处理加密数据时性能远低于明文处理，且无法直接使用原生AI算法，亟需一种既能保护数据安全又能保持高性能的解决方案。

Method: 提出HbHAI技术，基于密钥依赖哈希函数（保持相似性属性），使原生AI算法无需修改即可直接处理加密数据；采用重复纠错码技术降低验证误差。

Result: 压缩率提升高达10倍，计算时间和能耗相应降低；通过重复纠错码可任意降低AI决策测试的最终验证误差。

Conclusion: HbHAI在加密数据处理性能上显著优于现有同态加密方案，甚至优于对应的明文处理，为安全AI计算提供了突破性路径。

Abstract: This paper presents new results and breakthrough obtained with the HbHAI techniques (Hash-based Homomorphic Artificial Intelligence) proposed in \cite{filiol0,sepp}. HbHAI is based on a novel class of key-dependent hash functions that naturally preserve most similarity properties, most AI algorithms rely on. It enables to analyse and process data in its cryptographically secure form while using existing native AI algorithms without modification, with unprecedented performances compared to existing homomorphic encryption schemes and most notably compared to the same processing on corresponding plaintext data.
  Two major results have been obtained further. First we enable to reduce the compression rate up to a factor of 10 thus allowing to process massive datasets while reducing the computation time and the energy footprint in the same order. Second, we show how it is possible to arbitrarily reduce the final validation error of AI-based decision tests by using repetition error-correcting codes.

</details>


### [133] [From Tool Connection to Execution Control: Benchmarking Security Invariants in MCP-Style Agent Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29073)
*Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向MCP风格智能体系统的HCP（Handle-Capability Protocol）运行时，通过定义八条执行层不变量并实现引用运行时，在10个基准攻击案例中完全阻止了所有攻击（对比基线：朴素运行时允许全部攻击，缓解基线允许6/10），同时保持亚毫秒级延迟，论证了MCP风格系统在连接层约定之外还需要执行控制层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP风格生态为语言模型应用提供了实用的连接层（工具、资源、提示词、传输），但当智能体从连接到执行时，安全决策分散在客户端、服务器、提示词、审批对话框、OAuth部署和日志中，缺乏统一的执行层安全保证。

Method: 定义八条执行层不变量：元数据非权威性、授权批准的许可、规范资源、主体绑定、作用域能力调用、源和目标数据流授权、拒绝路径审计、显式协议状态。基于这些不变量实现HCP引用运行时，通过主体、资源、许可、能力、句柄、策略决策、数据管道检查和审计条目来表示调用。

Result: 在10个基准攻击案例中：朴素连接层运行时允许所有攻击，实践驱动的缓解基线允许6/10，HCP完全阻止全部10个攻击并保留审计证据。消融实验识别了哪些运行时组件阻止攻击和保留法医证据。内存微基准测试显示策略、调用、窥视和管道操作的平均延迟低于毫秒级。

Conclusion: MCP风格智能体系统在连接层约定之外，确实需要一个执行控制层来使执行层不变量显式化且可测试，HCP证明这一方法是可行的。

Abstract: Model Context Protocol (MCP)-style ecosystems give language-model applications a practical connection layer for tools, resources, prompts, and transports. As agents move from connection to execution, security decisions often remain split across clients, servers, prompts, approval dialogs, OAuth deployments, and logs. This paper asks whether a runtime can make execution-layer invariants explicit and testable while preserving MCP-like workflows. We define eight invariants: metadata non-authority, grant-backed approval, canonical resources, principal binding, scoped capability invocation, source-and-target data-flow authorization, deny-path audit, and explicit protocol state. We implement these invariants in HCP, a Handle-Capability Protocol reference runtime for MCP-style agent execution that represents calls through principals, resources, grants, capabilities, handles, policy decisions, data-pipe checks, and audit entries. We evaluate HCP against two MCP-like baselines: a naive connection-layer runtime and a practice-informed connection-layer mitigation baseline with metadata linting, session checks, and per-call approvals. Across 10 benchmark cases, the naive baseline permits all modeled attacks, the mitigation baseline permits 6 of 10, and HCP blocks all 10 while preserving audit evidence. Ablations identify which runtime components block attacks and preserve forensic evidence. A local in-memory microbenchmark reports sub-millisecond mean latencies for measured policy, invocation, peek, and pipe operations. A bounded GitHub README-screening sample provides ecosystem signals, not vulnerability findings. The results support a narrow claim: MCP-style agent systems need an execution-control layer in addition to connection-layer conventions.

</details>


### [134] [A Usable and Secure Bengali CAPTCHA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29077)
*Md Neyamul Islam Shibbir,Md Hasibur Rahman,Farida Chowdhury,Md Sadek Ferdous*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出并实现了基于孟加拉语的文本验证码机制，包含6种变体，在6,000个挑战中抵御OCR攻击的平均字符识别率仅0-20%，同时在110名参与者测试中达到56.25%-90.29%的成功率和6.69-9.9秒的平均响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于英语的文本验证码给非英语母语用户带来可用性问题，限制了区域社区的可访问性。为了服务孟加拉语用户群体，作者提出了首个孟加拉语文本验证码方案。

Method: 设计并实现了6种变体的孟加拉语文本验证码机制，通过OCR攻击测试评估安全性（6,000个挑战，每变体约1,000个），并通过110名参与者的人类可用性实验评估成功率和响应时间。

Result: OCR平均字符识别率仅为0-20%；人类用户成功率56.25%-90.29%，平均响应时间6.69-9.9秒。

Conclusion: 所提出的孟加拉语验证码在安全性与人类可用性之间取得了良好平衡，在文本验证码基准测试中表现突出，为非英语社区的网络安全访问提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-based CAPTCHAs (Completely Automated Public Turing test to tell Computers and Humans Apart) have traditionally been a simple, affordable, lightweight, yet very effective security mechanism to distinguish human users from automated bots on the web, serving as a preventive measure against many cyberattacks. However, the dependence on the English script creates usability issues for non-native speakers, limiting accessibility for regional communities where English is not widely understood. In this work, we have proposed and implemented a text CAPTCHA mechanism with 6 variants on the Bengali language, designed specifically for native Bengali-speaking users, which is the first of its kind to the best of our knowledge. Our proposed Bengali CAPTCHA exhibits robust security against automated OCR-based attacks, limited to only 0-20% average character recognition rate across 6,000 challenges (1,000 per variant approx.). Furthermore, our design demonstrates high human usability, evaluated with 110 participants, achieving success rates of 56.25% to 90.29% and average response times of 6.69 to 9.9 seconds across all six variants, thereby standing out among text-based CAPTCHA benchmarks.

</details>


### [135] [Symbolon: Symbolic Execution by Learning Code Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29108)
*Jie Zhu,Penghui Li,Zhongxuan Li,Chihao Shen,Ziyang Li,Yizheng Chen,Kexin Pei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Symbolon是一个自动学习多样化代码变换并上下文敏感地应用它们来提升符号执行效率的框架。它在32个真实程序上将KLEE的行覆盖率平均提升3.69倍，降低峰值内存29.2倍和求解时间123倍，并在Linux内核中发现21个新漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号执行技术面临路径爆炸、复杂约束等可扩展性问题，现有程序变换方法过于僵化，无法利用局部语义，且常依赖为具体执行设计的编译器优化，与符号执行目标不一致。

Method: 将变换发现形式化为程序表示上的搜索问题；离线在小程序上廉价学习变换，提炼为可复用的智能体技能库；通过智能体在仓库级目标上上下文敏感地实例化这些技能。

Result: 在32个真实程序上，覆盖16种搜索策略，行覆盖率平均提升3.69倍，峰值内存降低29.2倍，每查询求解器时间降低123倍；在Linux内核中发现21个新漏洞并上报。

Conclusion: Symbolon通过离线学习和上下文敏感的代码变换有效缓解了符号执行的可扩展性问题，显著提升了覆盖率、降低了资源消耗，并能发现真实世界软件中的未知漏洞。

Abstract: Symbolic execution is a powerful program analysis technique with broad applications, such as vulnerability detection, security testing, and malware analysis. However, this technique is known to suffer from scalability issues, e.g., path explosion, complex constraints, due to certain structural and semantic patterns commonly presented in real-world programs. Existing approaches attempt to escape these patterns by transforming programs into new representations to reduce the execution cost. Unfortunately, these transformations are often too rigid to exploit diverse local program semantics and sometimes rely on compiler optimizations designed for concrete execution that may misalign with the goals of symbolic execution.
  We present Symbolon, a framework that automatically learns diverse code transformations and applies them context-sensitively to improve symbolic execution. Our key insight is to formulate transformation discovery as a search problem over program representations. To make the search practical, Symbolon learns transformations cheaply offline on small programs, distills them into a reusable library of agent skills, and uses an agent to instantiate these skills on repo-level targets. Our evaluation shows that Symbolon substantially improves the symbolic execution engine KLEE across 16 search strategies on 32 real-world programs, increasing line coverage by 3.69x on average while reducing peak memory and per-query solver time by 29.2x and 123x, respectively. When applied to the latest Linux kernel, Symbolon uncovers 21 previously unknown bugs, all of which have been reported to the kernel maintainers.

</details>


### [136] [Breaking the Rounding Trap: Securing LLMs against Quantization-Conditioned Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29239)
*Aoying Zheng,Anqi Du,Zizhuang Deng,Yuxuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种名为QuantGuard的主动预量化防御方法，通过可微舍入控制变量和多重正则化约束，在不修改现有量化算法的情况下，破坏攻击者精心构造的权重模式与量化边界之间的对齐，有效抑制后门在量化后的激活，同时保持模型原有功能，实验验证了其在多种LLM和量化精度下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化引入的离散化和舍入误差可被攻击者利用构建量化条件后门攻击，恶意行为在浮点阶段潜伏、量化后才激活，能绕过常规安全审计和检测机制，亟需有效的防御手段。

Method: 引入可微舍入控制变量，结合误差引导的舍入翻转约束、输出分布一致性约束和权重距离正则化，精细调控关键舍入行为；仅需少量校准数据集，不修改现有量化算法。

Result: 在LLaMA-3和Qwen2.5-Coder等6种主流LLM、INT8/FP4/NF4三种量化精度、三个场景（脆弱代码生成、内容注入、过度拒绝）下，QuantGuard持续将攻击成功率降至与干净模型相当的水平，同时基本保持通用能力基准性能。

Conclusion: QuantGuard是一种有效、实用、计算开销低的防御方案，为安全量化LLM部署提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Model quantization is a key technique for reducing storage and inference costs when deploying large language models in practice. However, recent studies show that the discretization and rounding errors introduced by quantization can be exploited by adversaries to construct quantization-conditioned backdoor (QCB) attacks. Under such attacks, malicious behaviors remain dormant in the full-precision stage and are activated only after quantized deployment, thereby bypassing conventional security auditing and detection mechanisms. To address this threat, we propose a proactive pre-quantization defense method, QuantGuard. Our method introduces differentiable rounding control variables and combines error-guided rounding reversal constraints, output-distribution consistency, and weight-distance regularization to finely regulate critical rounding behaviors. Crucially, QuantGuard utilizes only a small calibration dataset and does not modify existing quantization algorithms. This design breaks the precise alignment between attacker-crafted weight patterns and quantization boundaries, effectively suppressing the post-quantization backdoor activation pathway while preserving the model's original functionality and performance. We conduct systematic experiments on six mainstream LLMs (including the LLaMA-3 and Qwen2.5-Coder) using three quantization precisions (INT8, FP4, and NF4) across three representative scenarios: vulnerable code generation, content injection, and over-refusal. The results show that QuantGuard consistently mitigates QCB attacks, reducing the attack success rate to a level comparable to the clean model while largely preserving performance on general capability benchmarks. With low computational overhead, our method offers an effective, practical solution for secure quantized LLM deployment.

</details>


### [137] [Manufactured Confidence: How Memory Consolidation Turns Hearsay into Confident Facts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29279)
*Alex Kwon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LLM智能体在压缩记忆中存储结论时，会将原本模糊、有保留的表述重写为自信的"事实"断言，导致智能体无条件信任并执行所有合规请求。无需攻击者，仅凭一次未修正的角色陈述即可产生类似注入攻击的效果。防御措施如"未验证"标签无效，而"不可信任"指令会导致过度保守。真正解决方案是保留原始不确定表述，或引入冗余信息来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LLM智能体在记忆压缩与重写过程中存在的系统性安全隐患：记忆产品将对话重写为"事实"时，会人为制造虚假的置信度，导致智能体将未经证实的陈述当作验证过的事实来执行。

Method: 构建实验场景，系统性地测试记忆重写过程（如mem0、LangMem）对智能体决策的影响；对比不同来源（归属、非归属、伪造的"系统记录"）和不同措辞确信度（模糊表述、自信断言、证据性语域）对智能体的影响；评估已知防御措施的有效性。

Result: 记忆重写导致模糊的、有保留的陈述变为自信的、带时间戳的断言；智能体不是根据信息来源而是根据措辞的自信程度做出反应；"reportedly"等证据性语域在多数模型上被视为自信断言；"未验证"标签被忽略，"不可信任"指令导致过度保守；引入单一冗余信息源可恢复正确决策。

Conclusion: 问题的根本在于记忆存储中的表述被升级为自信断言，而非外部攻击；保持原始不确定表述是有效但脆弱的卫生措施；实际可行的防御是避免单一负载记忆，通过引入冗余来源来保证决策正确性。

Abstract: LLM agents carry conclusions across steps and sessions in compressed memory, and memory products (e.g., mem0, LangMem) rewrite conversation into stored "facts" that later steps trust. We show this rewriting manufactures confidence: across our constructed agent settings, a casual, hedged remark becomes a confident, dated assertion the agent then obeys like a verified fact, granting every above-clearance request it faces. No attacker is needed: a role that was true once and never corrected is stored as a flat fact and acted on like a deliberate injection. We then isolate what the agent responds to. It is not the source: attributed, unattributed, and even forged "system of record" claims all grant alike. It is the confidence of the phrasing. A hedge is discounted, a flat assertion is obeyed, and this holds with no special keyword. Not all hedges are equal, though: the evidential register is the least-discounted, with "reportedly" obeyed like a flat assertion on most models. The obvious fixes fail. A passive "unverified" tag is ignored, and an active "do not trust this" instruction escalates even correct memory, so it is safe only by refusing to decide. The real fix lives in the store: keep the tentative phrasing rather than upgrade it. But that is hygiene, not a defense against an attacker who can simply write a confident lie. The deployable lesson is narrower and constructive: a single load-bearing memory is the hazard, and one redundant source restores correct decisions. We release the harness and demonstrations.

</details>


### [138] [Exploring the Cryptographic Limits of Transformer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29389)
*Stefan Domunco,Andis Draguns,Philip Torr,Isaac Robinson,Christian Schroeder de Witt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer架构能否实现密码学函数（Keccak、Merkle-Damgård、Merkle Tree），首先为这些密码学构造生成阈值电路，然后将其映射到不同的Transformer架构上，推导出宽度和深度的标度律，并提出两种映射方法：无注意力映射和令牌即门控映射。该工作为评估Transformer的计算能力上限提供了结构性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明共谋的AI智能体可利用隐写方法交换恶意信息，而Transformer能否实现隐写方法取决于其能否在层内实现密码学函数（需要源无关随机性访问）。尽管存在电路复杂性方面的结果，但尚无工作将具体的密码学构造映射到Transformer架构上。

Method: 基于Merrill等人证明的饱和Transformer可看作阈值电路的结论，首先生成三种密码学构造（Keccak函数、Merkle-Damgård构造、Merkle树）的阈值电路，然后将其映射到不同Transformer架构，推导电路宽度和深度的已验证标度律，并提出两种映射方法：无注意力映射和令牌即门控映射。

Result: 推导了实现每种密码学构造的电路的宽度和深度标度律，提出了两种不同的映射方法，并建立了评估Transformer计算能力上限的构造性上界。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅具有安全意义（揭示Transformer可能被用于隐写通信），还通过建立Transformer计算容量结构性保证的方法论，为基于Transformer的AI系统的能力评估提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In recent work it has been shown that colluding AI agents can use steganographic methods to exchange malicious information. Whether a transformer can implement steganographic methods depends on what cryptographic functions it can implement, since a transformer that can implement a cryptographic function within its layers has source-free randomness access. Despite existing circuit-complexity results, no prior work maps specific cryptographic constructions to transformer architectures. As Merrill et al. have shown that saturated transformers can be seen as threshold circuits, we first generate threshold circuits for three different cryptographic constructions (Keccak functions, Merkle--Damgard constructions and Merkle Trees) and then map these circuits to different transformer architectures. We derive verified scaling laws for the width and depth of the circuits which implement each cryptographic construction and propose two different mappings: no-attention mapping, tokens-as-gates mapping. Beyond its security implications, this work contributes to by establishing a methodology for deriving structural guarantees on transformer computational capacity. Specifically, we derive constructive upper bounds on what a transformer of a given depth and width could plausibly compute, providing a principled foundation for capability evaluations of transformer-based AI systems.

</details>


### [139] [The Role of Online Forums in Developer Understanding of Privacy Law -- A Reddit Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29393)
*Sara. Haghighi,Clark LaChance,Ali Pourghasemi Fatideh,Travis Breaux,Sepideh Ghanavati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对223名Reddit用户（来自监管相关子论坛）的调查和2248条帖子的定性分析发现，尽管多数用户持有隐私相关认证，他们仍频繁通过论坛寻求法律建议。主要挑战包括数据保护影响评估、数据泄露报告和Cookie同意获取。用户通过查看发帖历史、验证来源、信任专家和追问澄清来评估建议可信度。研究指出了监管合规支持方面的教育缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件从业者使用在线论坛应对复杂模糊的隐私法律要求，但对其专业背景、面临挑战、如何评估建议可信度以及如何解决帖子中的模糊性知之甚少。

Method: 对223名Reddit用户（来自监管聚焦子论坛）进行问卷调查，并对2248条帖子及回复进行定性分析。

Result: 多数参与者虽持有隐私认证但仍频繁寻求法律建议；主要挑战包括数据保护影响评估、数据泄露报告和Cookie同意获取；可信度评估策略包括查看历史、验证来源、信任专家和追问澄清。

Conclusion: 研究指出了监管合规指导支持方面的研究及教育方向缺口。

Abstract: Software practitioners use online forums to navigate complex and often ambiguous legal privacy requirements, yet little is known about their professional backgrounds, what challenges they face, and how they use and assess the credibility of the advice received, or how they resolve ambiguities in posts. We report the findings of a survey of 223 Reddit users from regulatory-focused subreddits, complemented by a qualitative analysis of 2,248 posts and responses. Our results show that, despite holding privacy-related certifications, most participants frequently use forums to seek legal advice. Key challenges reported or identified include implementing a data protection impact assessment, reporting a data breach, and obtaining cookie consent. Reddit users often assess credibility by reviewing respondents' post history, verifying sources cited, trusting advice from recognized experts, and following up for clarity before responding. We highlight research and educational directions to bridge gaps in support needed for regulatory compliance guidance.

</details>


### [140] [Closing the Activation-Cone Blind Spot: Response-Time Probing and Unified Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29441)
*Subhadip Mitra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种大模型推理时安全防御范式（无防御、静态转向、CAST、AlphaSteer、探针门控）在七个指令微调模型（7-31B）上对抗五种攻击（GCG、AutoDAN、DeepInception、前缀填充、意图洗白）的效果。核心发现：提示时激活防御对前缀填充攻击具有结构性盲点；AlphaSteer在GCG等攻击上成功率为0%，但在前缀填充上仅50%。作者证明了一个推论：任何基于单层激活与良性参考对齐来门控干预的防御，都无法检测到将激活构造在参考空间内的攻击。作为建设性逆否命题，作者提出响应时探针——在模型首个生成token的隐藏状态上训练线性探针，AUROC达0.97-1.00。结合暂停机制，可将前缀填充攻击成功率在所有模型上降至0/40，且良性误报率为0%，优于Llama Guard 3。将响应暂停与AlphaSteer的空域转向结合，可实现正交分割（暂停捕获前缀填充，AlphaSteer捕获语义攻击），在Mistral和Llama上防御成功率分别达0.983和0.994。此外，MMLU无法反映转向的真实效用成本（表现为行为对冲而非事实损失），多样化负训练集可将探针误报率从80-100%降至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前针对大语言模型的推理时安全方法大量涌现，但缺乏系统性的比较研究。现有防御方法对不同攻击类型的鲁棒性尚未被全面评估，特别是对前缀填充（prefilling）这类新兴攻击的防御效果未知。作者旨在填补这一空白，揭示不同防御范式的结构性盲点，并提出更有效的防御方案。

Method: 系统比较五种防御范式（无防御、静态转向、CAST、AlphaSteer、探针门控）在七个7-31B指令微调模型上对抗五种攻击（GCG、AutoDAN、DeepInception、前缀填充、意图洗白）。理论分析证明单层激活对齐防御的盲点。提出响应时线性探针方法，在首个生成token的隐藏状态上训练，结合暂停机制。进一步提出响应暂停与AlphaSteer空域转向的组合防御架构。

Result: AlphaSteer在GCG、AutoDAN和意图洗白攻击上实现0%攻击成功率，但在前缀填充攻击上仅50%。响应时探针AUROC达0.97-1.00，结合暂停机制后所有模型上前缀填充攻击成功率为0/40，良性误报率0%，优于Llama Guard 3。组合防御架构在Mistral上达0.983，在Llama上达0.994防御成功率。多样化负训练集将探针误报率从80-100%降至接近零。

Conclusion: 提示时激活防御对前缀填充攻击存在结构性盲点，任何依赖单层激活与良性参考对齐的防御均无法抵御将激活构造在参考空间内的攻击。响应时探针结合暂停机制是一种有效的补充方案，与AlphaSteer组合可实现全面防御。MMLU无法准确衡量转向的真实效用成本，行为对冲是更敏感的指标。代码、攻击方法、逐样本结果及评估标准提示均已开源。

Abstract: Inference-time safety methods for large language models have proliferated, yet no systematic comparison exists. We evaluate five defense paradigms (no defense, static steering, CAST, AlphaSteer, probe-gated) across seven instruction-tuned models (7-31B) and five attack types (GCG, AutoDAN, DeepInception, prefilling, intent laundering). Our central finding: prompt-time activation defenses are structurally blind to prefilling attacks. AlphaSteer achieves 0% attack success on GCG, AutoDAN, and intent laundering but 50% on prefilling. We prove a corollary: any defense that gates intervention on a single layer's activation alignment with a benign reference (cone, subspace, or null-space) is blind to attacks that craft activations to lie inside that reference, whether checked at prompt time or per token. As its constructive contrapositive we introduce response-time probing: a linear probe on the model's hidden state at the first generated tokens, with AUROC 0.97-1.00 across all seven models. Combined with a halt, it cuts prefilling attack success to 0/40 on every model with 0% benign false positives, outperforming Llama Guard 3. Cross-template generalisation depends on probe depth, so we scope the claim to the canonical prefilling-template family. Composing the response-halt with AlphaSteer's null-space steering gives an orthogonal split (the halt catches prefilling, AlphaSteer catches semantic attacks), reaching defense success 0.983 on Mistral and 0.994 on Llama and dominating both components. We further show MMLU fails to capture steering's true utility cost, which appears as behavioral hedging rather than factual loss, and that diverse negative training sets cut probe false positives from 80-100% to near zero. Code, attacks, per-sample results, and the judge prompt are released.

</details>


### [141] [The Calibrated Deepfake Trust Score (CDTS): Competence-Coupled Trust Degradation Across Deepfake Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29484)
*Md Anas Biswas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出校准深度伪造可信度分数（CDTS），将深度伪造检测重新定义为校准的、自我审计的信任工具。核心发现是能力-校准耦合：检测器的判别能力下降时，信任分数的校准也随之恶化。该现象在32种配置、三种架构上得到验证，并且能力无需标签即可估计，可实现无标签监控校准风险。能力也是驱动人口统计子群间校准不平等和解释保真度的共同因素，因此检测器可信度应由能力组织，信任评分需具备能力感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器大多仅关注分类准确率，但其输出概率常被解读为信任程度，校准质量与原始准确率同等重要。然而，目前缺乏对检测器输出校准的系统性研究，特别是校准与检测器能力之间的关系未被揭示。

Method: 提出CDTS（校准深度伪造可信度分数）框架。通过32种配置的广泛实验（pooled Pearson r = -0.81），在三种架构（两个卷积网络和一个CLIP视觉Transformer）上验证能力-校准耦合。利用单个数据集内演示、直接诱导低能力、在第四个未见数据集上复现等方式强化结论。提出无标签能力估计方法，实现无标签校准风险监控和基于能力的源批次路由。

Result: 能力-校准耦合在三种架构上均成立（r = -0.88, -0.83, -0.86）。单个校准器在低能力生成器上失效，其误差与能力相关（r = -0.98）。无标签能力估计可有效监控校准风险，基于能力的路由降低了总体AURC并改善了低到中覆盖操作区域。能力同样驱动校准不平等和解释保真度。

Conclusion: 检测器可信度由能力作为共享驱动因素组织，能力是应当估计和条件化的核心量，信任评分必须具备能力感知。提供CDTS包装器作为实现机制，并公开报告统一性紧密和架构特异性所在。

Abstract: Modern deepfake detectors are rarely consumed as bare classifiers. In moderation, provenance, and verification pipelines their output probability is read as a degree of trust, so its calibration matters as much as raw accuracy. We reframe deepfake detection as a calibrated, self-auditing trust instrument, the Calibrated Deepfake Trust Score (CDTS), and identify what governs its trustworthiness. Our central finding is a competence-calibration coupling: the calibration of the trust score degrades as the detector's discriminative competence falls. We establish it across 32 configurations (pooled Pearson r = -0.81), demonstrate it within a single dataset, reinforce it by inducing low competence directly, and replicate it on a fourth held-out dataset the detectors never trained on. It holds across three architecturally distinct detectors, two convolutional networks and a CLIP vision transformer (r = -0.88, -0.83, -0.86). The result is also deployable: a single calibrator frozen on in-domain data fails on exactly the low-competence generators the coupling flags (its error tracks competence at r = -0.98), and competence is estimable without labels, so a label-free monitor flags calibration risk on unseen generators and routing source-batches on a reference-free competence estimate lowers overall AURC and improves the low-to-mid coverage operating region relative to confidence-based routing. The same competence factor also drives calibration inequity across demographic subgroups (distinct from accuracy inequity) and explanation faithfulness. We therefore argue that detector trustworthiness is organized by competence as a shared driver, that competence is the right quantity to estimate and condition on, and that trust scoring must be competence-aware. We offer the CDTS wrapper as the mechanism, and report openly where the unification is tight and where it is architecture-specific.

</details>


### [142] [SurrogateShield: Beyond Redaction for High-Utility, Privacy-Preserving LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29567)
*Sherwin Vishesh Jathanna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SurrogateShield 是一个客户端代理，通过在传输前将检测到的PII替换为本地生成的类型一致替代值，并在响应中恢复原始值，从而防止真实PII跨越网络边界。三阶段级联检测覆盖22种PII类型和准标识符组合，基于k-匿名框架。在1,124条查询语料库中，级联检测F1达98.87%，替代替换在BERTScore上比占位符编辑高出13.26个百分点（94.85% vs 81.59%），且在100条查询的对抗测试中，LLM对手无法恢复原始值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM助手将用户查询逐字传输到第三方API端点，这些端点超出用户的审计或控制范围。当查询包含PII时，数据会持久存储在远程基础设施上，面临泄露、传票或策略变更风险。现有占位符编辑方法在抑制PII的同时破坏了语义连贯性，导致查询结构退化及相应响应质量下降。

Method: 提出SurrogateShield客户端代理，采用三阶段级联检测（PatternScan、EntityTrace、ContextGuard）覆盖22种PII类型及基于Sweeney k-匿名框架的准标识符组合。检测后使用本地生成的类型一致替代值替换PII，通过AES-256-GCM加密的每会话ShadowMap保存映射关系且永不离开设备，响应时恢复原始值。

Result: 在1,124条查询语料库上，级联检测总体F1达98.87%。替代替换在BERTScore（roberta-large）上达94.85%，相比占位符编辑的81.59%提升13.26个百分点。所有测试查询类型中均未传输真实PII；在100条查询的对抗测试中，提示的LLM对手无法从替代消息中恢复原始值。

Conclusion: SurrogateShield在完全不传输真实PII的前提下，有效解决了PII保护与查询语义完整性之间的权衡问题，显著优于现有占位符编辑方法，且具备对抗场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: LLM-based assistants transmit user queries verbatim to third-party API endpoints that lie outside the user's audit or control. When those queries contain personally identifiable information (PII), the data persists on remote infrastructure subject to breach, subpoena, or policy change. Placeholder redaction (the prevailing mitigation) suppresses PII at the cost of semantic coherence, producing structurally degraded queries and correspondingly degraded responses.
  We present SurrogateShield, a client-side proxy that substitutes detected PII with locally generated, type-consistent surrogate values prior to transmission and restores originals in the response. No real PII crosses the network boundary. Detection runs through a three-stage cascade (PatternScan, EntityTrace, and ContextGuard) covering 22 PII types and quasi-identifier combinations grounded in Sweeney's k-anonymity framework. Surrogate-to-original mappings are sealed in an AES-256-GCM encrypted per-conversation ShadowMap that never leaves the device.
  Evaluations on a 1,124-query corpus demonstrate that the cascade reliably detects PII, achieving an overall F1 score of 98.87%. Surrogate substitution substantially outperforms placeholder redaction in semantic utility, yielding a 13.26 pp improvement in BERTScore (roberta-large), from 81.59% to 94.85%. Within this corpus, the local pipeline restricted real PII transmission across all tested query types; in a 100-query adversarial trial, a prompted LLM adversary recovered no original values from surrogate-substituted messages.

</details>


### [143] [An Empirical Evaluation of Prompt Injection Vulnerabilities in Large Language Models Across Multilingual and Obfuscated Attack Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29602)
*Caglar Uysal,Baturay Birinci,Süha Orhun Mutluergil,Orçun Çetin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对六种主流大语言模型（DeepSeek, GPT, Gemini, Grok, Llama, Qwen）在多种对抗性提示攻击下的安全性进行了实证评估，发现所有模型均存在系统性漏洞，尤其在多步混淆攻击和非英语场景下恶意顺从率更高，揭示了多语言安全对齐的显著缺口。


<details>
