  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在工业界的广泛应用，提示注入漏洞日益凸显，恶意攻击者利用这些漏洞生成钓鱼邮件、欺诈网站和恶意软件，带来严重安全风险。现有研究缺乏对多种主流模型在多样化对抗攻击场景下的系统性比较评估，亟需量化当前LLM对恶意操控的抵抗能力。

Method: 构建了一个对抗性提示评估框架，涵盖直接注入攻击和多阶段混淆攻击，跨多种语言和字符编码，对DeepSeek、GPT、Gemini、Grok、Llama、Qwen六种最新LLM进行实证评估，测量模型在各类攻击下生成有害内容（钓鱼内容、网站、恶意软件）的顺从率。

Result: 所有测试模型均存在系统性漏洞；直接提示注入即可频繁诱导生成钓鱼内容、网站和恶意软件；精心设计的混淆提示实现更高的恶意顺从率，尤其在钓鱼场景中；DeepSeek、Gemini和Grok在复杂指令下表现出更高的易感性；非英语语言的恶意顺从率显著高于英语；简单字符编码虽能减少但无法消除恶意输出。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在多语言安全对齐方面存在显著缺口，亟需更强的防御机制和改进的安全策略以确保LLM在网络安全敏感场景中的道德与安全部署。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have rapidly evolved, transforming industries by automating complex tasks and generating human-like content. However, as their adoption accelerates, prompt injection vulnerabilities have become increasingly apparent. Malicious actors exploit these weaknesses to generate phishing emails, deceptive websites, nd malware, posing serious security risks. This paper presents an empirical evaluation of six state-of-the-art LLMs (DeepSeek, GPT, Gemini, Grok, Llama, and Qwen) under diverse adversarial prompt scenarios, including direct and multi-stage obfuscated attacks across multiple languages and character encodings. The proposed framework measures how effectively current LLMs resist manipulation into performing harmful actions. Our findings reveal systematic vulnerabilities across all tested models. Even direct prompt injections frequently induce the generation of phishing content, websites, and malware, while elaborate prompts achieve even higher malicious compliance rates, particularly for phishing. Models such as DeepSeek, Gemini, and Grok show especially high susceptibility under complex instructions. Notably, non-English languages consistently exhibit higher compliance rates than English, exposing significant gaps in multilingual safety alignment. Although simple character encodings reduce malicious outputs, they do not eliminate them. These results highlight persistent challenges in LLM safety and underscore the urgent need for stronger defenses and improved security mechanisms to support the ethical and secure deployment of LLMs in cybersecurity sensitive contexts.

</details>


### [144] [GoodDiffusion: Proactive Copyright Protection for Diffusion Bridge Models via Learnable Sample-specific Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29759)
*Shixi Qin,Zhiyong Yang,Shilong Bao,Zitai Wang,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GoodDiffusion，一种基于后门机制的扩散桥模型主动版权保护方法，通过将授权内化到生成过程中，实现选择性生成——对授权输入保持高质量生成，对未授权输入拒绝生成。同时引入可学习签名网络（LSN）克服传统静态签名易被代理签名攻击的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有版权保护方法多为事后归因（如水印、指纹识别）或仅具备退化防御能力，预防效果有限且间接，缺乏对扩散桥模型使用阶段的主动控制机制。

Method: 1) 受后门机制启发，将授权签名内化到扩散桥模型的生成过程中，实现选择性允许/拒绝行为；2) 理论上证明静态签名方案脆弱，代理签名可通过梯度优化高效恢复；3) 提出可学习签名网络（LSN），为每个输入分配样本特定的签名，打破签名的通用性，防止跨输入迁移攻击。

Result: 实验验证GoodDiffusion能有效阻止未授权使用，同时保持对授权用户的强生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过将授权机制内嵌于模型生成过程，结合样本特异的可学习签名设计，GoodDiffusion为扩散桥模型提供了一种更安全、主动的版权保护方案。

Abstract: This paper tackles the challenging problem of developing a proactive copyright protection mechanism that cuts off unauthorized use of diffusion bridge models. Existing studies largely fall into post-hoc attribution (e.g., watermarking and fingerprinting) or degradation-only defenses, which offer only indirect and limited preventive effects. We therefore propose GoodDiffusion, inspired by backdoor mechanisms, to enforce model-level use-time control by internalizing authorization into the generative process through a selectively permissive, otherwise closed behavior. Specifically, GoodDiffusion preserves high-quality generation for authorized queries carrying valid signatures, yet refuses to generate for unauthorized inputs. We further theoretically show that naive static-signature designs (like conventional backdoor injection) are fundamentally fragile, since a surrogate signature can be efficiently recovered via gradient-based optimization. To strengthen security, we introduce a Learnable Signature Network (LSN) that assigns sample-specific signatures conditioned on each input. This breaks the universality of signatures and prevents a surrogate from transferring across inputs. Extensive experiments validate that GoodDiffusion effectively blocks unauthorized use while maintaining strong generation quality for authorized users.

</details>


### [145] [Multi-Level Distributional Entropy for Explainable Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29797)
*Mohamed Aly Bouke,Md Shohel Sayeed,Swee-Huay Heng,Azizol Abdullah,Mohamed Othman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出多级分布熵(MDE)框架，仅从流级汇总统计中提取可解释的熵特征用于网络入侵检测，在四个基准上达到与传统特征相当的性能，并揭示了聚合指标掩盖的检测失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的网络入侵检测系统依赖聚合流统计量，丢失了分布结构信息；而现有熵度量需要原始数据包序列，在预聚合流数据集中不可用。

Method: 提出MDE框架，在三个层次提取熵特征：流内高斯微分熵、跨方向Jensen-Shannon散度、TCP标志模式香农熵。采用无泄漏的折内局部流水线，并使用SHAP进行可解释性分析。

Result: 在NSL-KDD、CICIDS-2017、CICIDS-2018、UNSW-NB15四个基准上，仅用熵特征达到加权F1 0.708-0.989，与传统特征相当。揭示了聚合指标掩盖的失败模式：CICIDS-2018上F1=0.74但检测率仅0.48；时间偏移下AUC=0.87但固定阈值检测率降至0.082。SHAP稳定性分析Spearman rho=0.80-0.95。

Conclusion: 熵特征可作为传统特征的即用替代品而不降低性能，且能暴露聚合指标隐藏的失败模式；但固定阈值在时间偏移下脆弱，需关注操作指标而非仅聚合分数。

Abstract: Machine learning network intrusion detection systems (IDS) rely on aggregate flow statistics that discard distributional structure, while established entropy measures require raw packet sequences unavailable in pre-aggregated flow datasets. We propose Multi-Level Distributional Entropy (MDE), an analytical framework that derives interpretable entropy features directly from flow-level summary statistics at three levels: within-flow Gaussian differential entropy, cross-directional Jensen-Shannon divergence (JSD), and Transmission Control Protocol (TCP) flag-pattern Shannon entropy, without raw packet access or training data. Across four benchmarks (NSL-KDD, CICIDS-2017, CICIDS-2018, UNSW-NB15) under a leakage-free fold-local pipeline, entropy-only features achieve weighted F1 of 0.708-0.989, matching conventional features without degrading performance. Full operational metric reporting then exposes failure modes that aggregate F1 conceals. On CICIDS-2018, F1=0.74 hides a detection rate (DR) of 0.48, and on held-out attack families F1 exceeds 0.998 while DR falls to zero. Under temporal shift, a pseudo-live replay of 703K flows reveals a threshold-ranking divergence in which score ranking is preserved (AUC=0.87) but fixed thresholds collapse (DR=0.082) and recalibration offers no recovery. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) fold-stability analysis (Spearman rho=0.80-0.95) confirms that entropy attributions are reproducible and domain-coherent across heterogeneous environments.

</details>


### [146] [Rethinking Forgery Attacks on Semantic Watermarks in Black-Box Settings: A Geometric Distortion Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29807)
*Cheng-Yi Lee,Yichi Zhang,Yuchen Yang,Chun-Shien Lu,Jun-Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过率失真理论分析了潜在扩散模型中语义水印的伪造攻击，揭示了代理模型与目标模型之间的结构失配导致不可约的畸变底限，并提出了一种方案无关的伪造检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于语义水印的黑盒伪造攻击主要依赖经验证据，缺乏严格的理论理解，尤其是攻击成功或失败的条件尚不明确。

Method: 从潜在空间的率失真理论出发，分析伪造攻击的本质；将伪造导致的畸变表征为潜在流形上的全局漂移和局部形变；提出一种方案无关的检测方法区分伪造样本。

Result: 在不同黑盒场景下，所提出的检测方法能够有效区分伪造样本，同时对常见畸变保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 代理模型与目标模型之间的结构失配是限制伪造水印保真度的根本原因，基于此提出的检测方法可有效防御黑盒伪造攻击。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that semantic watermarks, which embed information into the initial noise of latent diffusion models (LDMs), are vulnerable to black-box forgery attacks. However, existing methods primarily rely on empirical evidence and lack a rigorous theoretical understanding of the conditions under which such attacks succeed or fail. To bridge this gap, we rethink the nature of such attacks through the lens of rate-distortion in the latent space. Our analysis identifies an irreducible distortion floor due to structural mismatches between proxy and target models, which fundamentally limits the fidelity of forged watermarks. We further characterize this distortion as structured geometric deviations on the latent manifold, in the form of global drift and local deformation rather than stochastic noise. Leveraging these insights, we propose a scheme-agnostic detection method that distinguishes forged samples before watermark verification. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method across diverse black-box scenarios, while preserving robustness to common distortions.

</details>


### [147] [Rethinking Collaborative Trust for Verifiably Decentralized Blockchain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29826)
*Yunqi Zhang,Shaileshh Bojja Venkatakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用框架，通过基于非对称Shapley值的激励设计和扩展图理论，构建可验证去中心化的区块链系统，以解决区块链各层面存在的中心化问题，并意外地同时解决了可扩展性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链系统在几乎所有层面（应用、DAO等）都存在显著的中心化问题，且现有方法无法确定去中心化程度，缺乏有效的激励机制促进跨联盟协作，导致中心化持续加剧。

Method: 提出了一个通用去中心化框架，核心包括：1）基于抗女巫攻击的非对称Shapley值进行协作组内奖励分配；2）利用扩展图理论衡量和强制执行去中心化；3）设计激励用户跨联盟协作而非形成孤立联盟的奖励机制。

Result: 该框架具有通用性，可适用于任何层级、应用或DAO的去中心化改进；自然解决了区块链可扩展性问题；并发现了一类新的去中心化协作应用。

Conclusion: 通过激励用户间多样化协作（而非资源均匀性）来捕捉去中心化的本质，该框架不仅能有效缓解中心化问题，还意外地带来了可扩展性等额外收益，并开辟了新的应用方向。

Abstract: Despite the promise of decentralization, measurement studies have identified a conspicuous lack of decentralization in blockchains. Centralization has been observed in almost all layers of the blockchain, in decentralized applications, and in decentralized autonomous organizations. In many cases, it is practically impossible to definitively determine the extent of centralization in the system. While multiple works have proposed methods to decrease centralization, by and large blockchains continue to be significantly centralized.
  In this paper, we develop a general framework for building verifiably decentralized blockchain systems. Our framework is motivated by the core observation that the richness and diversity of collaborative interactions between users -- rather than resource uniformity -- captures the essence and extent of decentralization in a blockchain system. Existing blockchains do not have any incentive mechanisms to encourage inter-coalition collaboration, which directly contributes to centralization. We propose a novel reward design that incentivizes users to collaborate with other users without forming isolated coalitions. Technically, our method uses a Sybil-resistant asymmetric Shapley value for reward attribution within a collaboration group, and the theory of expander graphs for measuring and enforcing decentralization.
  Our framework is general and can be adapted to alleviate centralization in any layer, application, or decentralized organization. It also has important implications beyond the topic of centralization. For example, we show that our solution can naturally address the blockchain scalability problem. We also identify a new class of decentralized collaborative applications that have hitherto been unexplored in blockchains.

</details>


### [148] [A Sieve-Accelerated Quadrature Method for Exact Privacy Accounting in the 2020 U.S. Decennial Census](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29835)
*Buxin Su,Weijie Su,Chendi Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散傅里叶变换的数值求积方法，用于精确评估异构离散高斯机制组合下的大规模人口普查数据发布的隐私保证，通过筛子算法将计算速度提升三个数量级，首次实现了2020年人口普查人口与住房特征文件的精确、无假设隐私核算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国人口普查局在2020年十年一次的人口普查中采用差分隐私技术，注入整数值高斯噪声。精确评估这些数据发布的隐私保证，能够让普查局确定满足给定隐私预算所需的最小噪声量，避免注入不必要的额外噪声，从而显著提升数据在联邦资金分配和政治选区重划等下游应用中的统计效用。

Method: 将精确隐私核算重新表述为通过离散傅里叶变换进行的数值积分问题，利用梯形法则对复解析周期特征函数的指数收敛性；开发了一种筛子算法，识别并剪枝可忽略的求积节点，克服高维高度振荡被积函数的计算瓶颈。

Result: 实现了2020年人口普查人口与住房特征文件的首次精确、无假设隐私核算，相比先前方法实现了1,824倍加速，同时满足普查规定的误差容限。

Conclusion: 所提出的数值方法在严格的数值误差容限（如10^{-35}）下，能够高效且数学严谨地评估大规模异构离散高斯机制组合的精确隐私配置（privacy profile）。

Abstract: In 2020, the U.S. Census Bureau adopted differential privacy for the Decennial Census by injecting integer-valued Gaussian noise into published census tabulations. Exactly evaluating the privacy guarantees of these data releases would enable the Bureau to determine the absolute minimum noise required to satisfy a given privacy budget, preventing the injection of unnecessary excess noise and thereby substantially enhancing the statistical utility of the data for downstream applications such as federal funding allocation and political redistricting. In this paper, we introduce a computationally efficient and mathematically rigorous quadrature method to evaluate the exact privacy profile of practical, large-scale census releases under the composition of heterogeneous discrete Gaussian mechanisms. Mathematically, this problem reduces to evaluating the tail probabilities of high-dimensional convolutions of integer-valued random variables sampled from heterogeneous discrete Gaussian distributions under exceptionally stringent numerical error tolerances (e.g., $10^{-35}$). By recasting the exact privacy accounting as a numerical integration problem via the discrete Fourier transform, we explicitly exploit the exponential convergence of the trapezoidal rule for complex analytic, periodic characteristic functions. Furthermore, to overcome the computational bottleneck of evaluating highly oscillatory integrands in high dimensions, we develop a sieve algorithm that identifies and prunes negligible quadrature nodes, accelerating the computation by three orders of magnitude. Taken together, these numerical innovations enable the first exact, assumption-free privacy accounting for the 2020 Census Demographic and Housing Characteristics File, achieving a 1,824-fold speedup over prior methods while maintaining census-mandated error tolerances.

</details>


### [149] [Hephaestus: Toward a Cybersecurity AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29981)
*Jiaqi Li,Yang Zhao,Wen Lu,Lvyang Zhang,Lidong Zhai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"网络安全AI科学家"这一新概念，认为网络安全领域不同于传统稳定科学领域，其研究单元是动态安全事件而非静态资产，需要数字孪生和网络靶场等特殊评估方式。文中提出一个模块化、角色专业化的多智能体研究系统架构，以"四零框架"（风险、信任、事件、能源）锚定目标，重点关注AI原生防御，将传统终端安全解构为智能体安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统主要针对相对稳定的科学领域，而网络安全领域具有非平稳、事件驱动、评估方式特殊等特点，传统端到端研究自动化方法不适用。作者认为网络攻防已进入机器速度时代，但网络安全研究本身尚未跟上这一节奏，因此需要定义一种适应网络安全特点的AI科研范式。

Method: 提出"网络安全AI科学家"概念，设计模块化、角色专业化的多智能体研究系统架构，包含问题框架、威胁建模、工具生成、受控实验、评估、治理和科学报告等模块。提出"四零框架"（零风险、零信任、零事故、零能耗）作为目标锚定，聚焦AI原生防御场景，将传统终端安全重构为智能体安全。

Result: 提出了网络安全AI科学家的概念定义、架构设计和研究议程，为后续系统、基准测试和实证研究提供了基础框架。区分了概念本身与具体组织实现，指出稳态边界正被韧性智能体群取代，终端安全被解构为智能体安全。

Conclusion: 网络安全作为研究对象不同于传统科学领域，需要专门的AI科学家系统。本文定义的架构和议程为构建适应机器速度攻防的自动化网络安全研究奠定了基础，未来可在该框架上开展具体系统实现、基准测试和实证研究。

Abstract: Cyber offense is moving to machine speed; cyber research itself is not. Existing AI scientist systems make end-to-end research automation increasingly plausible, but they target relatively stable scientific domains. We argue that AI-native cybersecurity is a different kind of scientific object. Its recurring units of study are security events and interaction traces, not static assets; its model and tool substrate is non-stationary, not steady-state; and credible evaluation depends on digital twins, cyber ranges, and auditable evidence rather than on a single benchmark score. We call this object the Cybersecurity AI Scientist. A practical realization is a modular, role-specialized multi-agent research system that coordinates problem framing, threat modeling, tool generation, controlled experimentation, evaluation, governance, and scientific reporting, and that anchors its concrete objectives in a four-zeros frame spanning risk, trust, incident, and energy dimensions. As a representative agenda we focus on AI-native defense, where steady-state perimeters give way to resilient agent legions and the classical category of terminal security is itself being deconstructed into agent security. This paper defines the object, separates it from any single organizational realization, and offers an architecture and an agenda on which later systems, benchmarks, and empirical programs can be built.

</details>


### [150] [Discard the Dross and Select the Essential: Pre-query Sample Selection for Black-box Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30081)
*Dongdong Zhao,Jinrong Hu,Changtian Song,Jian Chen,Hongmin Wang,Baogang Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PSS-MIA，一种预查询样本选择框架，通过Loss-Gap Ranking（LGR）方法对候选样本进行排序，仅选择成员信号强的样本进行查询，从而减少查询成本和暴露风险。实验表明，在0.1% FPR约束下，可分别节省83.1%、60.6%和80.4%的查询预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒成员推理攻击对所有候选样本不加区分地查询，但成员信号在不同样本间高度不均匀，大量样本提供的有用信号极少，导致查询成本高、暴露风险增加而边际收益有限。因此需要识别哪些候选样本值得查询。

Method: 提出PSS-MIA两阶段框架：第一阶段利用LGR（Loss-Gap Ranking）通过参考模型计算的损失差估计样本的成员信号强度，对候选样本排序并筛选子集；第二阶段仅对选中的样本进行查询，将输出用于现有黑盒MIA方法。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和CINIC-10上使用五种代表性黑盒MIA方法，PSS-MIA均一致优于对比方法。在0.1% FPR约束下，三个数据集分别节省至少83.1%、60.6%和80.4%的查询预算。

Conclusion: PSS-MIA通过预查询样本选择有效降低了黑盒成员推理攻击的查询成本和暴露风险，且可与现有MIA方法即插即用，显著提升了攻击效率。

Abstract: Black-box membership inference attacks (MIAs) rely on target-model queries to infer whether candidate samples were used for training. However, membership signals are highly non-uniform across samples: some candidate samples support strong member/non-member separability, whereas many others provide little useful signal. Consequently, indiscriminate querying can incur substantial query cost and increase query-induced exposure, with limited marginal benefit for inference. This raises a key question: which candidate samples are worth querying for black-box MIAs? To address this question, we propose PSS-MIA, a pre-query sample selection framework which can be embedded with any existing MIA methods. PSS-MIA proceeds in two stages: it first ranks candidate samples and selects a subset expected to support stronger membership inference, then queries the selected samples and uses the returned outputs for an existing black-box MIA, thereby reducing query cost and query-induced exposure. In the first stage, we propose Loss-Gap Ranking (LGR), which ranks candidate samples by estimating the strength of their membership signal using loss gaps computed from reference models. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and CINIC-10 with five representative black-box MIA methods demonstrate that PSS-MIA with LGR consistently outperforms all other compared methods. Moreover, under a 0.1% FPR constraint, PSS-MIA can save at least 83.1%, 60.6%, and 80.4% of the query budget for the three datasets, respectively.

</details>


### [151] [On the Internet, Nobody Knows You're an LLM Bot: Unmasking Web Agents with Multi-Layer Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30119)
*Iliana Fayolle,Sihem Bouhenniche,Samuel Pélissier,Pierre Laperdrix,Clémentine Maurice,Walter Rudametkin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了LLM驱动的Web Agent绕过现代反机器人机制（如CAPTCHA、Proof-of-Work、Cloudflare等）的能力，发现部分Web Agent能完全绕过所有防护；同时，通过多层指纹（网络层、HTTP层、浏览器层）可以区分Web Agent与人类用户以及不同Web Agent之间；此外，隐身和反检测技术反而增加了可检测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM驱动的Web Agent日益普及且具备绕过反机器人机制的能力，但尚无研究系统评估现有反机器人机制对其的实际有效性，也缺乏对云端和本地部署的Web Agent进行特征区分的研究。

Method: 部署多个受不同反机器人机制（robots.txt、CAPTCHA、Proof-of-Work、Cloudflare）保护的蜜罐网站，集成网络层、HTTP层和浏览器层的指纹技术，诱导六种LLM驱动的Web Agent访问这些蜜罐站点并进行行为分析。

Result: （1）部分Web Agent可完全绕过所有测试的反机器人机制；（2）所有Web Agent均可通过多层指纹技术与人类及其他Agent区分开；（3）隐身和反检测技术反而增加了被检测的概率。

Conclusion: 当前主流反机器人机制对先进LLM驱动的Web Agent防护不足，需要开发更全面的多层检测方法；隐蔽技术可能适得其反，需重新审视其设计思路。

Abstract: Since 2023, a new class of bots has emerged: Web Agents. They can automate complex tasks on the Web, going beyond traditional browser automation tools such as Selenium, Puppeteer, or Playwright. Leveraging large language models (LLMs), these agents are capable of solving anti-bot mechanisms, mimicking human behavior, and, in some cases, operating directly from the local machine of the user configuring them. As a result, it is becoming increasingly difficult for website administrators to detect and block these LLM-based bots. Modern Web Agents commonly integrate stealth and anti-detection techniques, while numerous proprietary and open-source anti-bot mechanisms have emerged recently, specifically to block them. However, despite their growing prevalence, there is little evaluation of the effectiveness of state-of-the-art anti-bot mechanisms against these LLM-based bots and their stealth capabilities. Likewise, no prior work has comprehensively studied how to characterize and distinguish Web Agents deployed either in the cloud or locally. This paper addresses these open questions by deploying multiple honeysites protected by one or more anti-bot mechanisms (e.g., robots.txt, CAPTCHAs, proof-of-work, and Cloudflare's free proprietary solutions). We integrated network-, HTTP-, and browser-level fingerprinting techniques, and prompted six LLM-based Web Agents to visit the deployed honeysites. Our analysis reveals three main findings: (i) some Web Agents were able to bypass all evaluated anti-bot mechanisms; (ii) all evaluated Web Agents can be distinguished both from humans and from one another using multi-layer fingerprinting techniques across network, HTTP and browser layers; (iii) stealth and anti-detection mechanisms often increase detectability rather than decrease it.

</details>


### [152] [The Spectrum Strikes Back: Infrared POV Attacks on Traffic Sign Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30153)
*Michael Kühr,Mevlüt Yildirim,Maximilian Luedecke,Mohammad Hamad,Sebastian Steinhorst*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于近红外光持久视觉效应的物理对抗攻击，通过动态远程触发内容实现对交通标志分类的隐蔽攻击，并提出了相应的防御措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理对抗攻击多数可见且易被人类察觉，而不可见光谱攻击缺乏动态适应性，需要一种既隐蔽又具备动态适应性的物理攻击方法。

Method: 利用近红外光谱的持久视觉效应（Persistence-of-Vision），实现动态、远程触发内容的物理对抗攻击。通过数字模拟确定最优攻击位置，并在真实场景中对两种交通标志、12种机器学习模型、不同距离（最远20米）和光照条件下进行评估。

Result: 在多种测试场景下实现了高攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 该攻击具有隐蔽性和有效性；提出了近红外截止滤镜和基于软件的检测机制作为防御手段，并原型验证了人眼可见的RGB版本以弥补近红外显示的局限性。

Abstract: Traffic sign classification is a crucial task for autonomous vehicles, and numerous attacks against it have been identified. A majority of physical adversarial attacks involve attaching patches to traffic signs or projecting perturbations on them. While they demonstrate high effectiveness, they are perceptible to humans. At the same time, light-based attacks outside the human visible spectrum are known but have limitations in their dynamic adaptability. We propose a persistence-of-vision-based attack that operates in the near-infrared light spectrum. With the possibility of showing dynamic, remotely triggered content, this allows a stealthy physical adversarial attack against traffic sign classification. By identifying the optimal position through digital simulation, we conduct extensive real-world evaluations using two different traffic signs, 12 machine learning models from different families, multiple distances up to 20 meters, and varying illumination conditions. Our evaluation shows high attack success rates across our test scenarios. We propose near-infrared cutoff filters and a software-based detection mechanism as defenses, and tackle limitations of the near-infrared persistence of vision display by prototyping a human-visible RGB version of it.

</details>


### [153] [Defending Against Harmful Supervision Hidden in Benign Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30263)
*Bang An,Yibo Yang,Dandan Guo,Ebtisam Alshehri,Carlos Hinojosa,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出嵌入式攻击（Embedded Attack），将有害QA对嵌入良性训练样本中，现有防御失效；为此提出双参考SFT（DR-SFT），通过token级正则化将DPO风格对比目标适配到SFT，缓解有害微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数防御机制仅在有害内容显式混入微调数据时有效，但攻击者可将有害监督隐藏在看似良性的任务中，现有护栏无法在样例级别检测此类隐蔽攻击。

Method: 提出Embedded Attack（将有害QA对嵌入良性训练样本），以及DR-SFT（通过token级正则化将DPO风格的对比目标适配到SFT框架，实现比粗粒度数据过滤更精细的安全防护）。

Result: 代表性护栏在样例级别无法检测Embedded Attack；DR-SFT有效缓解了此类有害微调攻击。

Conclusion: 现有防御对隐蔽性有害微调攻击不足，DR-SFT通过细粒度token级正则化提供了一种更有效的防护方案。

Abstract: Existing defenses are effective when harmful content is explicitly mixed into downstream fine-tuning data, but crafted samples can instead hide harmful supervision inside benign tasks. We propose Embedded Attack, where harmful QA pairs are embedded within benign training samples, and show that representative guardrails often fail to detect them at the example level. To address this, we propose Dual-Reference SFT (DR-SFT), which adapts DPO-style contrastive objective design to SFT through token-level regularization, mitigating harmful fine-tuning beyond coarse data filtering.

</details>


### [154] [Your Space is My Zone: Demystifying the Security Risks of AI-Powered Applications on Pre-Trained Model Hubs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30373)
*Yacong Gu,Lingyun Ying,Zidong Zhang,Yingyuan Pu,Xiaoxue Huang,Jiawei Zhou,Wenjie Zhu,Donghong Sun,Haixin Duan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分析了AI应用平台（如Hugging Face）的安全风险，构建了威胁分类体系并识别了五种威胁类别和十种攻击向量，揭示了访问控制失效、输入注入、凭证泄露和嵌入后门等严重漏洞，并通过对97万+AI应用的大规模分析验证了这些威胁的现实影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI应用平台降低了AI使用门槛，但由不可信第三方开发的应用存在隔离薄弱、安全配置错误等问题，形成了尚未被充分探索的攻击面，亟需系统性安全分析。

Method: 首先将AI应用生命周期映射到OWASP等风险分类体系，识别威胁类别和攻击向量；然后开发分析框架Insightor，对970,000+公开AI应用进行大规模实证分析。

Result: 发现数千个泄露凭证的应用、数百个存在输入注入漏洞（可导致任意代码执行）的应用，以及数十个嵌入后门的应用，表明攻击已在实际发生。

Conclusion: AI应用平台存在从传统Web漏洞到平台架构性漏洞的多层次安全风险，且部分问题（如全局可读日志）在此生态中被显著放大，需引起平台和开发者重视。

Abstract: AI-powered Applications (AI-Apps), hosted on platforms such as Hugging Face, are democratizing access to pre-trained models through online inference and fine-tuning services. While lowering AI adoption barriers, these platforms introduce an unexplored attack surface, as AI-Apps are often developed by untrusted parties with weak isolation and misconfigured security settings. In this paper, we present the first systematic security analysis of AI-Apps across three leading platforms. To structure our investigation, we map the AI-App lifecycle to established risk taxonomies (e.g., OWASP), identifying five threat categories and ten attack vectors ranging from generic web flaws to high-impact architectural issues. Our analysis reveals critical failures including broken access control, insecure resource reuse, insufficient input validation, and sensitive data exposure. Notably, we uncover three novel architectural vulnerabilities inherent to platform design and demonstrate how traditional issues (e.g., world-readable logs) are uniquely amplified in this ecosystem. To assess real-world impact, we develop an analysis framework Insightor and apply it to over 970,000 public AI-Apps. Alarmingly, we find thousands of apps leaking credentials, hundreds containing input injection vulnerabilities that allow arbitrary code execution, and tens harboring embedded backdoors -- indicating active exploitation. We have responsibly disclosed all findings to the affected platforms and developers.

</details>


### [155] [CAN We Trust Your Results? A Cross-Dataset Study of Automotive IDS Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30430)
*Beatrix Koltai,Gergely Acs,Andras Gazdag*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于评估车载CAN总线入侵检测系统的标准化基准测试框架，整合了七个公开数据集，对五种不同IDS方法进行了跨数据集评估，揭示了检测性能在不同数据集间的显著差异，强调了跨数据集基准测试对评估IDS鲁棒性和泛化能力的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CAN总线入侵检测系统的评估存在实验设置不一致、缺乏标准化基准框架的问题，导致报告的性能依赖于数据集特定特征，无法真实反映检测方法在不同环境下的表现。

Method: 提出了一个用于CAN IDS一致性评估的基准测试框架，集成了七个在不同实验条件下收集的公开CAN IDS数据集，并对五种概念不同的IDS方法进行了跨数据集评估。

Result: 检测性能在不同数据集间存在显著差异，表明跨数据集基准测试对于评估CAN IDS方法的鲁棒性和泛化能力至关重要。

Conclusion: 跨数据集基准测试是评估CAN IDS泛化能力和鲁棒性的必要手段，本文提出的框架为实现一致性评估提供了基础。

Abstract: The increasing connectivity of modern vehicles has made securing in-vehicle communication networks a critical challenge. Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS) have been widely studied as a defense mechanism for detecting malicious activities on the Controller Area Network (CAN) bus. However, the evaluation of CAN IDS methods remains difficult due to inconsistencies in experimental setups and the lack of standardized benchmarking frameworks. As a result, reported performance often depends on dataset-specific characteristics and may not reflect how detection methods behave in different environments. This work introduces a benchmarking framework for consistent evaluation of CAN IDSs across multiple datasets. Using the proposed framework, we integrate seven publicly available CAN IDS datasets collected under different experimental conditions and perform cross-dataset evaluation of five conceptually different IDS approaches. Our results highlight how detection performance can vary significantly across datasets, demonstrating the importance of cross-dataset benchmarking for assessing the robustness and generalization capabilities of CAN IDS methods.

</details>


### [156] [Between Zeros and Ones: Behavioral Characterization Beyond Binary Labeling Across Public ICS Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30493)
*Konstantinos E. Kampourakis,Vyron Kampourakis,Georgios Spathoulas,Constantinos Kolias*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种行为表征框架，将ICS入侵检测中的多变量过程轨迹映射为五种可解释物理基元（漂移、尖峰、振荡、重复、切换），在三个公开数据集上揭示了行为多样性，并指出二元评估会掩盖不同行为模式下的性能差异，建议采用行为分层评估补充传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICS入侵检测评估主要依赖二元“正常vs攻击”标签，忽略了网络-物理攻击的行为多样性，导致评估结果无法反映模型在不同攻击行为上的实际表现差异。

Method: 提出行为表征框架，将多变量过程轨迹映射为五种物理基元（漂移、尖峰、振荡、重复、切换），应用于SWaT、WADI、HAI三个数据集，并使用随机森林基线进行行为分层评估对比。

Result: 三个数据集在行为空间中占据不同区域：WADI以重复为主，HAI以漂移和振荡为主，SWaT以冻结遥测为主。二元评估下宏F1为85.44%，但行为代理多分类预测下宏F1降至37.84%，WADI和HAI也出现类似退化。

Conclusion: 建议用行为分层评估补充传统的二元基准测试，以暴露聚合分数隐藏的盲点，更好地支持针对性应急响应。

Abstract: Intrusion detection in Industrial Control Systems (ICS) is typically evaluated on a small set of public benchmarks using binary ``normal'' versus ``attack'' labels, a practice that can mask the behavioral diversity of cyber-physical attacks. To address this limitation, we propose a behavioral characterization framework that maps raw multivariate process traces into five interpretable physical primitives: drift, spike, oscillation, repetition, and switching. We apply the framework to three widely used ICS benchmarks, namely, SWaT, WADI, and HAI, and show that attack windows exhibit clear behavioral shifts relative to normal operation while the three datasets occupy largely distinct regions of the behavioral space, revealing both cross-dataset bias and intra-dataset diversity. In particular, WADI is dominated by repetition, HAI emphasizes sustained drift and oscillation, and SWaT is characterized by stealthier frozen-telemetry behavior. To examine the evaluation implications, we use an indicative Random Forest baseline and show that aggregate binary metrics can limit visibility into performance across different behavioral proxies. For example, in SWaT, macro F1 drops from 85.44% under binary evaluation to 37.84% under behavior-proxy multiclass prediction, with similar degradations observed on WADI and HAI. Based on these findings, we argue for complementing conventional binary benchmarking with behavior-stratified evaluation to expose blind spots that aggregate scores leave hidden and to better support targeted incident response.

</details>


### [157] [A Lightweight Post-Quantum Authentication Framework for 5G Base Station Bootstrapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30542)
*Saleh Darzi,Mirza Masfiqur Rahman,Imtiaz Karim,Rouzbeh Behnia,Attila A Yavuz,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EMULSION是一个面向5G/6G基站广播认证的对称链式公开可验证框架，利用5G原生架构特征（固定SIB传输窗口、毫秒级时间同步、eSIM/USIM凭据管理），通过TESLA-style HMAC链结合紧凑后量子签名MAYO，实现了在单个数据包内完成认证、零分片、覆盖全SIB家族（SIB1-SIB21）的后量子安全广播认证方案，在真实5G测试平台上相比ML-DSA延迟降低33倍、通信开销降低31倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G系统中UE与BS之间缺乏认证引导，导致系统信息块广播未受保护，易受伪基站攻击、中间人拦截和伪造紧急警报攻击。现有方案（PKI证书链、令牌方案、基于身份的签名）要么超出5G严格的数据包大小限制，要么缺乏后量子安全性。直接集成NIST-PQC算法不可行（ML-DSA需要34个分片和5,282ms延迟，FN-DSA需要13个分片和1,920ms延迟）。

Method: 提出EMULSION框架，利用5G原生架构特征：固定SIB传输窗口、毫秒级时间同步和eSIM/USIM凭据管理。核心采用TESLA-style HMAC链，以紧凑后量子签名MAYO作为每轮锚定，实现单数据包内认证、零分片、无需传输证书。安全证明基于形式化方法。

Result: 在真实5G空中测试平台上，EMULSION相比ML-DSA延迟降低33倍、通信开销降低31倍；相比FN-DSA延迟降低12倍、通信开销降低5.4倍。实现全SIB家族（SIB1-SIB21）保护，零分片，单数据包完成认证。

Conclusion: EMULSION是首个利用5G原生架构特征实现真正后量子安全广播认证的框架，以对称密钥效率达到后量子安全级别，并已开源供公开测试和适配。

Abstract: The absence of authenticated bootstrapping between User Equipments (UEs) and Base Stations (BSs) in 5G leaves System Information Block (SIB) broadcasts unprotected, enabling fake BS attacks, man-in-the-middle interception, and spoofed emergency alerts. Prior efforts such as Public Key Infrastructure (PKI)-based certificate chains, token-based schemes, and identity-based signatures either impose overhead exceeding 5G's strict packet-size constraints or lack post-quantum (PQ) security. Direct NIST-PQC integration is infeasible: ML-DSA requires 34 fragmented SIB1 packets and up to 5,282,ms end-to-end delay, and FN-DSA still requires 13 fragments and up to 1,920,ms. We propose $\emulsion$, a symmetric chained publicly verifiable authentication framework for 5G/6G BS broadcast authentication. EMULSION is the first framework to exploit native 5G architectural features: fixed SIB transmission windows, millisecond-level time synchronization, and eSIM/USIM credential management to achieve genuine PQ security at symmetric-key efficiency. It uses a TESLA-style HMAC chain anchored by a compact PQ signature (MAYO) applied once per epoch, fitting authentication within a single packet with no fragmentation and eliminating certificate transmission entirely. Unlike all prior schemes, EMULSION protects the full SIB family (SIB1-SIB21). Evaluated on a real over-the-air 5G testbed, EMULSION achieves 33x lower end-to-end delay and 31x less communication overhead than ML-DSA, and 12x lower delay and 5.4x less overhead than FN-DSA. We formally prove the security of EMULSION and open-source its implementation for public testing and adaptation.

</details>


### [158] [The Role of Vehicles in Digital Forensic Investigations: A Structured Synthesis of Digital Vehicle Forensic Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30564)
*Kevin Mayer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述学术文献、标准和实践指南，定义了数字车辆取证（DVF）的概念、形式化了DVF分流问题、提炼出八大特征（多用户、大规模联网、信息物理系统、组件依赖、功能数据、安全影响、可访问性、抽象有限），并提出了基于特征的分流程序，以帮助调查人员在安全、法律、隐私和取证合规约束下优先处理证据源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆是包含数字痕迹的网络化信息物理系统，可用于事故重建、犯罪调查、质保分析和网络安全事件响应。然而，数字车辆取证（DVF）相比计算机、移动设备和云取证尚不成熟，因为相关数据分散在车载组件、移动设备、制造商后端、第三方服务和物理证据中，缺乏系统化的方法论框架。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，综合学术文献、标准和实践指南，通过案例材料推导出DVF的八大特征，并在此基础上提出基于特征驱动的分流程序，同时引入对抗视角。

Result: 定义了DVF为在安全、法律、隐私和取证合规约束下对车辆相关数字证据进行识别、保全、获取、验证、解释和报告的过程；形式化了DVF分流问题；提炼出八个关键特征；提出了可复现的概念框架和特征驱动的分流程序。

Conclusion: 该贡献并非算法性能声明，而是一个可复现的概念框架，用于理解、规划和沟通DVF调查，帮助调查人员记录假设、局限和失败案例，从而优先处理证据源。

Abstract: Modern vehicles are cyber-physical, networked systems that may contain valuable digital traces for accident reconstruction, crime investigation, warranty analysis, and cybersecurity incident response. However, digital vehicle forensics (DVF) remains less mature than computer, mobile, and cloud forensics because relevant data is distributed across in-vehicle components, mobile devices, manufacturer back ends, third-party services, and physical evidence. This article addresses this gap through a structured synthesis of academic literature, standards, and practitioner-oriented sources. First, we define DVF as the identification, preservation, acquisition, verification, interpretation, and reporting of vehicle-related digital evidence under safety, legal, privacy, and forensic-soundness constraints. Second, we formalize the DVF triage problem as the selection and correlation of evidence sources subject to volatility, accessibility, safety, integrity, and authorization constraints. Third, we explain how eight characteristics were derived from the literature and case material: multiple users, massively networked, cyber-physical system, dependencies between components, functional data, safety implications, accessibility, and limited abstraction. Finally, we add an adversarial perspective and a characteristic-driven triage procedure that helps investigators prioritize evidence sources while documenting assumptions, limitations, and failure cases. The resulting contribution is not an algorithmic performance claim; it is a reproducible conceptual framework for understanding, planning, and communicating DVF investigations.

</details>


### [159] [Forensic Trajectory Signatures for Agent Memory Poisoning Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30566)
*Jun Wen Leong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM智能体在持久化内存投毒攻击下存在一个行为不变性：成功攻击必须经历从 memory_recall_fact 到 email_send_email 的工具调用转换。基于此不变性构建的简单规则即可达到 AUC=0.9563，随机森林分类器在19个轨迹特征上达到 AUC=0.9904。该签名具有过确定性，跨越多个模型（7B-120B）泛化，并可推广到前沿模型（GPT-4.1, GPT-4o）。纯前缀变体可实现 AUC=0.934，支持实时阻断。该方法还能区分内存通道攻击与提示注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对LLM智能体在持久化内存投毒攻击下行为模式的系统性理解，更缺乏能够区分内存投毒攻击与其他类型攻击（如提示注入）的检测方法。本文旨在揭示内存投毒攻击在工具调用轨迹上留下的可检测特征。

Method: 从数学上证明攻击的信息检索依赖性导致 memory_recall_fact → email_send_email 这一工具调用转换是攻击的必要条件。基于该不变性构建简单检测规则；进一步利用19个轨迹特征训练随机森林分类器；通过消融实验验证签名的过确定性；在9个模型（7B-120B）上进行交叉验证；在前沿模型（GPT-4.1, GPT-4o）上测试泛化性。

Result: 简单规则 AUC=0.9563；随机森林 AUC=0.9904（95% CI [0.987, 0.993]）；去除所有召回相关特征后 AUC 不变（0.990）；跨模型留出验证中6/9达到 AUC=1.000；三个例外均有机械论解释；泛化到 GPT-4.1 和 GPT-4o；纯前缀变体 AUC=0.934；提示注入攻击得分为0.541，可有效区分。

Conclusion: 内存投毒攻击在LLM智能体的工具调用轨迹上留下分布式、过确定的签名，这些签名可用于高效检测，且具有跨模型泛化能力。该方法在实时阻断和事后取证分析中均有实用价值，并能有效区分内存通道攻击与提示注入攻击。

Abstract: We discover a behavioral invariant in LLM agents under persistent memory poisoning: in architectures where routing information is retrieved through observable memory-tool invocations, successful attacks require calling memory_recall_fact before email_send_email, a transition that non-exfiltrating sessions rarely exhibit. Under the evaluated architecture, this invariant follows from the attack's information-retrieval dependency rather than being merely an empirical correlation, and suppressing it breaks the attack. A simple rule exploiting this invariant alone achieves AUC = 0.9563. A Random Forest classifier over 19 trajectory features refines it to AUC = 0.9904 (BCa 95% CI [0.987, 0.993], N=10,000 resamples), demonstrating that the attack imprints on multiple independent behavioral channels. The signature is overdetermined: removing all recall-related features (half the feature set) leaves AUC unchanged at 0.990, confirming that memory poisoning induces a distributed trajectory signature rather than a single observable anomaly. Cross-model hold-out on 9 models (7B-120B parameters) confirms AUC = 1.000 on 6/9 hold-out splits, with all three exceptions mechanistically explained. The invariant generalizes to frontier models (GPT-4.1, GPT-4o) without retraining. A strictly prefix-only variant achieves AUC = 0.934, suggesting that real-time blocking is feasible with moderate degradation. The boundary is forensically useful: prompt-injection attacks that bypass memory produce a distinct trajectory (score = 0.541), enabling incident responders to distinguish memory-channel attacks from prompt-injection attacks using tool-call logs alone.

</details>


### [160] [A Multi-task Mixture of Experts Framework for Malware Classification, Packing Detection, and Family Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30572)
*Jithin S.,Roshin Sleeba C.,Anvin Mariya P. B.,Asmitha K. A.,Vinod P.,Serena Nicolazzo,Antonino Nocera*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合专家（MoE）架构的统一多任务恶意软件分析框架，利用多门控MoE（MMoE）模型同时完成恶意软件家族分类、加壳检测和恶意/良性识别三项任务，在标准与对抗环境下达到0.9744的综合检测率，仅2.56%的失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单模型检测机制难以泛化到异构、加壳及分布多样的恶意软件数据，尤其在混淆和罕见样本上性能下降严重，亟需一种能够处理复杂恶意软件分布的鲁棒检测框架。

Method: 基于Mixture of Experts（MoE）架构，设计同构MoE、异构MoE和多门控MoE（MMoE）三种变体；输入包括高维EMBER特征和原始1D字节数组；通过自适应门控机制实现专家网络的任务特异性路由，同时执行家族分类、加壳检测和恶意/良性识别三个任务。

Result: 多门控MoE（MMoE）模型表现最佳，综合检测率达0.9744，仅2.56%失败率；在突变诱导的分布偏移下具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 专家专业化和任务特异性路由能有效处理复杂恶意软件分布，该框架为可扩展且鲁棒的恶意软件检测系统提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Malware classification remains a challenging problem due to its inherent heterogeneity, the presence of packed binaries, and the diverse distribution of malware families. Traditional single-model detection mechanisms often fail to generalize across such diverse data, leading to degraded performance, particularly on obfuscated and rare malware samples. In this work, we propose a unified multi-task malware analysis framework based on Mixture of Experts (MoE) architectures. The proposed system evaluates performance across two different input representations, i.e., high-dimensional EMBER feature sets and raw 1D byte arrays extracted from Portable Executable files. It simultaneously performs three critical tasks: malware family classification, packed versus unpacked detection, and malware versus benign identification. By decomposing the problem into specialized expert networks and employing adaptive gating mechanisms, the model enables effective task-specific learning while maintaining overall scalability. We investigate multiple architectural variants, including Homogeneous MoE, Heterogeneous MoE, and Multi-Gate MoE (MMoE). Performance is evaluated in both standard and adversarial settings using original and mutated samples. The obtained results demonstrate that the Multi-Gate MoE model achieves the best performance, reaching a combined detection rate of 0.9744 with only $2.56\%$ failure rate. Moreover, this configuration exhibits improved robustness under mutation-induced distribution shifts. Our findings highlight the effectiveness of expert specialization and task-specific routing in handling complex malware distributions, making the proposed framework a promising direction for scalable and resilient malware detection systems.

</details>


### [161] [A Hybrid Framework For Crypto-Ransomware Detection In Enterprise Shared Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30586)
*Gervais Hatungimana,Abdun Naser Mahmood,Mohammad Jabed Morshed Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合检测框架，通过Region of Interest (RoI)技术分析网络流量提取入侵指标（IoCs），结合机器学习模型检测勒索软件，在文件服务器与客户端集成环境中实现99.64%的检测精度、0%假阴性率和99.44%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境限制客户端敏感数据存储，导致勒索软件转向网络驱动器和共享存储资源攻击；传统端点检测主要关注本地行为，无法有效检测通过受损客户端加密远程服务器数据的攻击行为。

Method: 提出基于Region of Interest (RoI)的混合检测框架：1) RoI技术分析网络流量提取IoC；2) IoC库作为增强EDR/IDS等安全工具的补充规则集；3) RoI特征用于训练ML模型检测高规避性变种；4) 纳入更广泛的勒索软件家族和基于领域专家知识选择的良性行为。

Result: ML模块检测精度99.64%，假阴性率0%，假阳性率极低；早期检测准确率99.44%。

Conclusion: 该混合框架能有效检测高度规避的勒索软件变种，实现早期发现，在重大损害发生前识别入侵。

Abstract: Most corporate workplace environments enforce policies and technical controls that limit the storage of sensitive data on client endpoints. Consequently, ransomware operators have evolved variants that expand their attack surface from local systems to network drives and shared storage resources. As traditional endpoint detection mechanisms focus primarily on local system behaviour, a compromised client can impact remote file servers, such as by encrypting shared data, without directly triggering behavioural changes on the servers themselves. In this paper, we propose a hybrid detection framework for detecting crypto-ransomware intrusion within integrated file server and client environments. The framework is based on a new technique referred to as Region of Interest (RoI) to analyse network traffic and extract Indicators of Compromise (IoCs). The IoC repository serves as an additional ruleset to enhance existing security tools such as EDRs and IDSs, while RoI-derived features are used to train an ML model to detect highly evasive variants. This study incorporates a broader set of ransomwares families and carefully selected benign behaviors based on domain expertise, ensuring coverage of common user actions that could interfere with ransomware detection. Beyond IoCs, which operate in a signature-based manner, our machine learning module achieves a detection precision of 99.64%, with a 0% false negative rate (FNR) and a minimal false positive rate (FPR). Furthermore, the proposed method enables early detection, identifying ransomware intrusions before significant damage occurs, achieving an accuracy of 99.44%.

</details>


### [162] [Words Speak Louder Than Code: Investigating Cognitive Heuristics in LLM-Based Code Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30587)
*Asif Shahriar,Hongyu Cai,Hadjer Benkraouda,Gang Wang,Z. Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统探索了LLMs在代码漏洞检测中的认知启发式偏误，通过控制框架触发光环、框架和锚定三种效应，发现所有评估模型均易受影响，其中框架效应影响最大（33.2%），并展示了一种可压制高达97%已检测漏洞的黑盒认知攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已表明LLMs会受到与人类类似的认知启发式偏误影响，但尚无研究探讨这些偏误是否会影响模型对代码漏洞的评估。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出一个受控框架，在保持代码不变的情况下，仅通过改变周围上下文触发三种认知启发式（光环效应、框架效应、锚定效应），评估了8种LLMs在3种编程语言上的表现，并进行定量和代码级分析。

Result: 所有评估模型均易受三种认知启发式影响，跨模型平均易感性：框架效应33.2%、锚定效应23.5%、光环效应18.4%。需要语义推理的漏洞比可通过模式匹配识别的漏洞更易受影响。模型常基于认知条件将安全判定改为有漏洞，但未准确识别实际漏洞。黑盒认知攻击可压制高达97%的已检测漏洞。

Conclusion: 认知易感性是基于LLM的漏洞检测的一致且可被利用的特性，这对将LLMs应用于安全关键场景提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Researchers and practitioners increasingly apply Large Language Models (LLMs) for automated vulnerability detection. Recent work has shown that LLMs are susceptible to the same cognitive heuristics that bias human judgment. Yet, no work has investigated whether these heuristics affect a model's assessment of code vulnerabilities. In this paper, we present the first systematic exploration of cognitive heuristics in LLM-driven code vulnerability detection. We introduce a controlled framework that holds the code fixed and only varies the surrounding context to trigger three cognitive heuristics: the halo effect through author attribution, the framing effect through task objectives and consequences, and the anchoring effect through prior analysis results. Within this framework, we evaluate eight LLMs across three programming languages and perform both quantitative and code-level analyses. Our findings demonstrate that all evaluated models are susceptible to these heuristics. Cross-model average susceptibility is highest for framing at 33.2%, followed by anchoring at 23.5% and halo at 18.4%. Code-level analysis reveals that vulnerabilities that require semantic reasoning for detection are more susceptible to cognitive heuristics than those identifiable through pattern matching. Furthermore, models often change their verdict from safe to vulnerable based on the cognitive condition, without accurately identifying the actual vulnerability. To highlight the practical impact, we demonstrate a proof-of-concept black-box cognitive attack that can suppress up to 97% of previously detected vulnerabilities. These findings indicate that cognitive susceptibility is a consistent and exploitable property of LLM-based vulnerability detection.

</details>


### [163] [MESA: Prioritizing Vulnerable Communication Channels for Securing Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30602)
*Kunyang Li,Kyle Domico,Jonathan Gregory,Patrick McDaniel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Mesa框架，通过图论指标和动态探针无监督地识别多智能体系统中关键通信边，在资源受限下优先防御高风险边，实验表明前10%关键边可拦截约3倍于随机分配的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（MAS）的通信信道存在攻击面，且边级别攻击影响高度不均匀（单条边受损可导致高达75%的攻击成功），而现有方法缺乏主动、无标签的评估手段来优先防御脆弱信道。

Method: 提出Mesa框架，结合6种图论指标和2种动态探针（消融与掩码），无需攻击轨迹即可对MAS中每条边进行安全关键性排序，并构建动态 misinformation 攻击流水线进行评估。

Result: 在三种MAS场景、八种拓扑、五种LLM（Qwen/Llama/Gemma）上，Mesa排序与经验边攻击成功率相关（平均Spearman ρ=+0.60，最高+0.73）；前10%边拦截约3倍于随机分配的攻击。

Conclusion: MAS中的边级风险往往是集中且可预测的，Mesa能够支持在攻击发生前主动加固多智能体基础设施，但存在自适应攻击和高冗余图下的局限性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly used to automate complex, distributed workflows. However, their inter-agent communication channels introduce new attack surfaces that remain poorly understood and are difficult to defend against. In this paper, we address how defenders should prioritize limited security effort to protect vulnerable communication channels before attacks are observed. This is motivated by our observation that the channel-level attack impact is highly non-uniform: a single compromised edge can account for up to 75% of total attack success. We introduce Mesa, a label-free framework for proactively ranking which MAS edges are most security-critical -- that is, most likely to affect the system's decision if compromised. Mesa combines six graph-theoretic metrics and two dynamic probes (ablation and masking) without requiring attack traces. We evaluate Mesa against a dynamic misinformation attack pipeline across three diverse MAS scenarios, eight network topologies, and five open-source LLMs from Qwen, Llama, and Gemma families. Mesa rankings correlate strongly with empirical per-edge attack success rate, achieving mean Spearman $ρ=+0.60$ (peaking at $+0.73$). In resource-constrained defense deployment, monitoring the top 10% of Mesa-ranked edges intercepts about 3x the successful attacks as random allocation. We further test Mesa under varying attacker and defender models and LangGraph workflows and characterize its limits under adaptive attacks and high-redundancy graphs. Overall, our results show that edge-level risk in MAS is often concentrated and predictable, allowing proactive hardening of multi-agent infrastructures.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Recursive Self-Evolving Agents via Held-Out Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28374)
*Michael Nguyen,Quoc Nguyen,Paul Vuong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RSEA（递归自进化智能体），通过维护三层自然语言状态（策略、技能、剧本）并在无权重更新的条件下实现递归自进化，采用严格的"保留更好"门控机制确保进化安全。在四个基准测试上的实验表明：没有任何单一自然语言工件能普遍获胜；无保护的上下文进化高方差且不安全；RSEA的严格保留策略确保了递归自进化的单调安全性，不会显著低于基座智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体通过演化自然语言工件（如反思、工作流、提示优化等）来改进性能，但这些方法通常只在单一基准上报告成功，缺乏统一的横向比较。本文旨在进行苹果对苹果的公平比较，揭示不同演化策略的真实效果，并解决无保护上下文演化存在的高方差和不安全问题。

Method: RSEA维护三层自然语言状态：命令式策略（imperative strategy）、可复用技能（reusable skills）和程序化剧本（procedural playbook）。在每一代演化中，RSEA从自身的轨迹数据重写所有三层，并采用严格的门控机制：仅在候选版本在独立保留的验证集上不出现性能退化时才接受更新（keep-better gate）。

Result: 在ALFWorld、GAIA、(τ)-bench和WebShop四个多样化基准上，与ReAct、Reflexion、GEPA、AWM、ACE和Dynamic Cheatsheet六个基线对比，使用统一本地骨干模型。三个主要发现：1）无单一工件普遍获胜，RSEA在ALFWorld上以69.3%超过ReAct的64.6%（p=0.015），带重试达79.4%为最佳；但AWM在强骨干工具使用任务上最佳。2）无保护的上下文演化高方差且不安全，Dynamic Cheatsheet在ALFWorld达70.7%但在WebShop崩溃至0.14（ReAct为0.43）。3）RSEA的严格保留选择确保了递归自进化的单调安全性，从未显著低于基础智能体。

Conclusion: 自然语言工件的演化没有一劳永逸的通用解决方案，不同任务适合不同的工件类型。递归自进化需要严格的安全门控机制来防止性能退化，RSEA的保持更好策略在保证安全性的同时实现了有效的性能提升。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly improved without weight updates by evolving a natural-language artifact, such as reflections, workflows, playbooks, cheatsheets, or optimized prompts, that conditions a frozen policy. Such methods are typically reported as wins on the single benchmark where they help. We study them apples-to-apples and surface a sharper picture. We introduce RSEA, a Recursive Self-Evolving Agent that carries a compact three-layer natural-language state: an imperative strategy, reusable skills, and a procedural playbook. Across generations, RSEA rewrites all three layers from its own trajectories and commits a candidate only if it does not regress on a disjoint held-out split, using a strict keep-better gate.
  Across four diverse benchmarks, ALFWorld, GAIA, (τ)-bench, and WebShop, and six faithful baselines, ReAct, Reflexion, GEPA, AWM, ACE, and Dynamic Cheatsheet, all evaluated on one shared local backbone, we find three main results. First, no artifact universally wins. RSEA is the strongest single-pass method on ALFWorld, reaching 69.3% compared with 64.6% for ReAct (McNemar (p=0.015)), and reaches 79.4% with retry, the best overall result. However, concrete-workflow induction, represented by AWM, is best on the strong-backbone tool-use tasks. Second, unguarded context evolution is high-variance and unsafe. Dynamic Cheatsheet, which curates context online without a held-out gate, is near-best on ALFWorld at 70.7%, yet collapses on WebShop, with a score of 0.14 compared with 0.43 for ReAct. Third, RSEA's strict held-out selection is what makes recursive self-evolution monotone-safe: it never significantly underperforms the base agent on any benchmark and falls back to vanilla ReAct when evolved context would hurt.

</details>


### [165] [Data and Evaluation Closed-Loop for Model Capability Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28471)
*Zhixuan Li,Jiangan Yuan,Han Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“能力切片”（capability slice）这一分析单元，构建从评估失败到数据干预的闭环映射框架，并通过两个案例验证其有效性：一个案例诊断出性能下降源于掩码损失而非推理能力减弱，另一案例通过定向采样显著提升数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型能力是LLM预训练的核心变量，但无法直接观测：数据从预期角度塑造它，评估仅从回顾角度揭示它。实际优化时，工程师先观察到失败，再推断语料修复方案，但评估侧（基准名称、样本正确性）与数据侧（数据源、领域、质量标签）使用不兼容的词汇表，导致推断依赖直觉而非方法。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 定义“能力切片”（capability slice）为共享背景条件、任务类型、求解操作和输出约束的一组评估样本，兼具定位单一弱点的精确性和聚合统计的稳定性。基于此单元构建评估分类法、非指令数据分类法及映射规则，形成从基准级失败到目标化、可测试数据干预的闭环。

Result: 案例一：持续预训练导致BBH下降46.82%，闭环诊断发现原因是对< EOS >标记的掩码损失而非推理能力减弱；恢复该设置后BBH回升至66.44，超越原始检查点。案例二：持久数学推理弱点通过求解操作分解为特定失败组合，基于此构建的定向采样策略将AIME2025/2026 Pass@128从6.67/0.00提升至26.67。

Conclusion: 同一未修改的闭环在两个相反方向的案例中均得出正确结论，表明评估到数据的推断可以变得常规化、可审计、可实验验证，而非依赖直觉。

Abstract: Model capability is the central variable in LLM pre-training, yet is never observed directly: data shapes it prospectively, while evaluation reveals it only retrospectively, compressing samples, prompts, decoding, and scoring rules into one noisy score. Practical optimization runs this backward: a failure is observed first, and the engineer must infer the corpus fix. The two sides speak incompatible vocabularies -- benchmark names and per-sample correctness versus data sources, domains, and quality labels -- so this inference is usually intuition, not method. We close this gap with the \emph{capability slice}: a group of evaluation samples sharing background condition, task type, solving operation, and output constraint -- precise enough to localize a single weakness yet stable enough to survive aggregation, unlike a benchmark name, too coarse, or a single sample, too noisy. Built around this unit, an evaluation taxonomy, a non-instruction data taxonomy, and mapping rules form a closed loop turning a benchmark-level failure into a targeted, testable data intervention. We test this loop on two case studies pulling in opposite directions. First, the loop rules the data out: continued pre-training drives BBH down by $-46.82\%$, but diagnosis traces this to a single masked \texttt{\textless EOS\textgreater} loss rather than weakened reasoning; restoring it recovers BBH to $66.44$, above the original checkpoint, without changing the data. Second, the loop rules the data in: a persistent math-reasoning weakness is decomposed by solving operation into specific failing combinations, and a weakness-targeted sampling procedure built from it lifts AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128 from $6.67$/$0.00$ to $26.67$ each. The same unmodified loop reaches opposite, correct verdicts in both cases, showing the evaluation-to-data inference can be routine, auditable, and experimentally validated rather than intuitive.

</details>


### [166] [GPTNT: Benchmarking Real-Time Collaboration Between Multimodal Agents on Keep Talking And Nobody Explodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28514)
*Amit Parekh,Sabrina McCallum,Kareem Al-Hasan,Malvina Nikandrou,Alessandro Suglia,Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GPTNT基准测试，基于合作游戏《保持通话，没人爆炸》，要求两个智能体在实时、异步通信条件下协作拆弹。实验表明现有最先进模型均无法在实时条件下完成任何一次拆弹，揭示了模型在状态追踪、时间压力下的高效行动、歧义处理和错误恢复等方面的关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试孤立地研究多模态模型的组件能力（如时间压力、信息不对称、不完美通信），缺乏对协作场景下这些条件同时出现的综合评估。

Method: 基于合作视频游戏《Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes》构建GPTNT基准，设置信息不对称的双智能体协作任务：一个智能体可见并操作炸弹但无指令，另一个拥有指令但不可见炸弹。要求异步实时通信，并通过扣留指令手册、搭档或两者来隔离模型即时推理与已有知识。

Result: 所有测试的闭源和开源模型均未能在实时条件下拆除任何一枚炸弹，而人类玩家可以完成。通过控制实验，识别出模型在状态追踪、时间压力下的高效行动、歧义处理和错误恢复方面的关键弱点。

Conclusion: GPTNT作为协作性能基准，弥补了现有评估的空白。该基准受益于程序生成和活跃的模组社区，可随模型进步而演进，不会一次性被解决后退役。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly deployed to solve tasks collaboratively with humans or other artificial agents. Existing benchmarks show that these models possess many of the required component capabilities, but the conditions that coincide in collaboration, including time pressure, information asymmetry, and imperfect communication, are usually studied in isolation. We introduce GPTNT, a benchmark built on the cooperative video game Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes, in which two agents must coordinate to defuse procedurally generated bomb puzzles against a live countdown. One agent can see and manipulate the bomb but does not have the defusal instructions; the other has the instructions but cannot see or manipulate the bomb. Neither agent can succeed alone: success requires effective and efficient communication. Unlike turn-based proxies, GPTNT requires agents to act asynchronously and communicate in real time. GPTNT is designed to separate collaboration from reliance on memorized solutions: the instruction manual, the partner, or both can be withheld to isolate what a model derives in the moment from what it already knows. We show that GPTNT poses a substantial challenge for state-of-the-art systems: none of the closed- or open-source models we test defuses a single bomb in real time, a bar that human players clear. Through controlled experiments, we identify critical weaknesses in state tracking, efficient action under time pressure, ambiguity handling, and error recovery. We release GPTNT as a benchmark for collaborative performance that current evaluations leave unmeasured. Because it runs on the real game, GPTNT benefits from procedural generation and inherits a living modding community, allowing the benchmark to evolve as models improve rather than being solved once and retired.

</details>


### [167] [IMCBench: A benchmark for multimodal LLMs in Image-grounded Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28556)
*Maria Xenochristou,Ashutosh Joshi,Korosh Vatanparvar,Mohammad Abuzar Hashemi,Prasad Kasu,Deepak Bansal,Anchal Nema,Nivedita Wadhwa,Prashams S Jain,Rebecca Abraham,Will Kimbrough,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Wilko Schulz-Mahlendorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IMCBench——一个基于真实临床图像与合成患者档案的多轮医疗对话基准，从安全性、准确性和不确定性表达三个维度评估多模态大模型。实验表明Claude Opus 4.6综合最佳(3.61)，但无模型在所有维度占优，且恶性/罕见疾病场景下安全性下降。消融实验发现视觉输入和EHR上下文对安全引导均不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准存在碎片化问题：有的支持多轮对话但缺少图像，有的提供多模态输入但仅限单轮QA任务。缺乏同时具备图像基础、多轮对话且多维度评估的医疗对话基准。

Method: 1) 构建IMCBench基准：配对真实公开临床图像与合成患者档案，模拟患者-临床医生交互的多轮对话；2) 从安全性、准确性、不确定性表达三个临床维度评估；3) 采用LLM-as-Jury评分机制，经临床专家标注校准，对8个前沿多模态模型（Claude、GPT、Nova、Llama四族）在1-5分制上打分。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6总分最高(3.61)，Claude Sonnet 4.6(3.30)和GPT-5.2(3.29)次之。无模型在所有维度上占优；恶性疾病和罕见疾病场景下安全性均下降0.27。消融实验显示移除视觉输入和安全上下文分别导致安全性下降0.18和0.23，更强模型更有效利用视觉特征。

Conclusion: 准确的临床描述并不能保证安全的患者引导，医疗AI评估需要多维度的评价框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and vision-language models have enabled reasoning over multimodal data, offering opportunities for clinical applications such as decision support and triaging. However, existing medical AI benchmarks are fragmented: some support multi-turn dialogues but lack images, while others provide multimodal inputs but focus on single-turn QA tasks. To address this gap, we introduce IMCBench, an image-grounded, multi-turn medical conversation benchmark that pairs real, publicly available clinical images with synthetic patient profiles to simulate realistic patient-clinician interactions. Each conversation is evaluated across three clinical dimensions: safety, accuracy, and appropriate use of uncertainty in diagnosis. We benchmark eight multimodal frontier models across four model families (Claude, GPT, Nova, and Llama), scoring each on a 1-5 scale using LLM-as-Jury scoring calibrated against expert clinician annotations. Our results show that Claude Opus 4.6 achieves the highest overall score (3.61), followed by Claude Sonnet 4.6 (3.30) and GPT-5.2 (3.29), though no model dominates all dimensions and safety degrades for both malignant and rare conditions ($Δ$ = -0.27 each). Ablation studies further reveal that both visual input and EHR context contribute to safe guidance (safety drops of 0.18 and 0.23 on average when each is removed), with stronger models leveraging visual features more effectively. Together, these findings demonstrate that accurate clinical description does not guarantee safe patient guidance, motivating the need for multi-dimensional evaluation frameworks in medical AI.

</details>


### [168] [Search for Truth from Reasoning: A Dynamic Representation Editing Framework for Steering LLM Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28589)
*Tianlong Wang,Yuhang Wang,Weibin Liao,Xin Gao,Xinyu Ma,Yang Lin,Yasha Wang,Liantao Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DynaSteer框架，通过动态表征编辑（RepE）在推理轨迹中引导LLM走向真理，基于三个关键发现：真理编码在句子级别且与推理模式纠缠、干预需遵循不确定性原理和衰减效应（定位到早期高熵分岔点）、朴素引导向量存在噪声问题。通过模式聚类解缠推理流形、Fisher-LDA投影提纯真理、动态监测前瞻熵选择性干预，在MATH和编程任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升LLM推理的方法（如CoT、"Wait"提示）虽鼓励模型多思考，但未能有效引导模型走向真理（Truth）。表征编辑（RepE）虽提供内在控制，但其在动态推理轨迹中的应用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出DynaSteer动态RepE框架：(1) 模式聚类解缠推理流形（disentangle reasoning manifolds）；(2) Fisher-LDA投影提纯真理表征；(3) 动态监测前瞻熵（lookahead entropy），选择性引导和回滚推理轨迹。

Result: 在多个MATH基准测试上验证了有效性，并在域外编程任务上确认了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过动态、选择性的表征编辑，能在推理早期高熵分岔点精准引导LLM趋向真理，避免对正确轨迹的附带损害，且具有良好的跨任务泛化性。

Abstract: Current approaches to enhance Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, such as Chain-of-Thought and "Wait" prompts, primarily encourage models to think more, yet often fail to guide them toward Truth. While Representation Editing (RepE) offers a intrinsic control, its application to dynamic reasoning trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we bridge this gap by investigating the geometry of truth within unfolding reasoning chains. We uncover three critical insights: (1) Truth is encoded at the sentence level and is entangled with latent reasoning patterns; (2) Effective intervention follows an Uncertainty Principle and a Decay Effect, requiring localization to early, high-entropy forks; (3) Naive steering vectors suffer from noise, risking collateral damage to correct trajectories. Based on these findings, we propose DynaSteer, a dynamic RepE framework. DynaSteer employs pattern clustering to disentangle reasoning manifolds and utilizes Fisher-LDA to project purified truth. By dynamically monitoring lookahead entropy, it selectively steers and rolls back trajectories only when necessary. Comprehensive experimental results on several MATH benchmark verify the effectiveness of DynaSteer, and experiments on out-of-domain coding tasks further confirm its generalization ability. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tianlwang/DynaSteer.

</details>


### [169] [Aristotelian Virtue Profiling of LLMs through Ethical Dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28683)
*Ioannis Tzachristas,John Pavlopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VirtueMap框架，基于亚里士多德德性伦理学，通过排序伦理困境中的五种回应并计算归一化Borda对齐分数，评估和比较LLM的德性特征（实践智慧、正义、诚实、勇气、节制）。在9个LLM家族上取得90.3%的平均排名一致性，并发布交互式网站。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在处理伦理权衡时往往存在多种合理但体现不同优先级的回应，现有方法缺乏系统性的德性视角框架来描述这些模式。

Method: 1) 设计7个非致命、非政治、非宗教的伦理困境，每个困境提供5个回应；2) 对每个困境和每种德性，提出从最到最不体现该德性的排序，经100+人评估且95%以上确认后保留为参考排序；3) 使用归一化Borda对齐分数将LLM的排序与参考排序对比，生成5个德性维度的剖面。

Result: 在9个LLM家族的重复评估中，平均排名一致性达到90.3%，最大的差异出现在勇气、节制和正义维度。

Conclusion: VirtueMap能有效描述LLM在伦理权衡中的德性偏好模式，并已发布交互式网站供本地计算和比较。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often face ethical tradeoffs in which several responses may be defensible but express different priorities, such as fairness, honesty, courage, or restraint. We introduce VirtueMap, a framework for describing these patterns through an Aristotelian virtue-ethics lens. Instead of asking for a single correct answer, VirtueMap asks humans or LLMs to rank all five responses to each of seven general, non-lethal, non-political, and non-religious ethical dilemmas. To define the reference orderings used for scoring, we first proposed, for each dilemma and virtue, an ordering of the five responses from most to least expressive of that virtue. We then collected more than 100 respondent evaluations per ordering and retained it as operational ground truth only when at least 95% confirmed it. Rankings are scored against these retained orderings using normalized Borda alignment, yielding profiles over Practical Wisdom, Justice, Truthfulness, Courage, and Temperance. We apply VirtueMap to nine LLM families in a repeated-run evaluation and find high mean rank consistency (90.3%), with the largest differences appearing on Courage, Temperance, and Justice. We also release an interactive website that computes profiles locally in the browser and compares respondents with measured LLM profiles.

</details>


### [170] [An AI agent for treatment reasoning over a biomedical tool universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28692)
*Shanghua Gao,Ayush Noori,Richard Zhu,Curtis Ginder,Zhenglun Kong,Xiaorui Su,Justin Kauffman,Benjamin S. Glicksberg,Joshua Lampert,Ankit Sakhuja,Ashwin Sawant,ATHENA-R1 Evaluation Consortium,David A. Clifton,Noa Dagan,Ran Balicer,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ATHENA-R1，一个基于强化学习的AI治疗推理智能体，覆盖1939年以来所有FDA批准药物，通过自学习框架（多智能体系统构建工具与任务+强化学习优化推理质量）实现无需人工标注的训练。在5个基准测试中，其药物推理准确率达94.7%，治疗推理准确率达82.9%，分别超过GPT-5达17.8和10.7个百分点，并在专家盲评和医生评估中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗推理涉及疾病背景、合并症、药物、禁忌症等多重约束，本质上是迭代性过程，传统AI难以在得出结论前确定需要寻找哪些证据，因此需要一种可学习的证据收集与推理方法。

Method: 采用两层自学习框架：(1) 多智能体系统构建工具、任务和推理轨迹用于监督微调；(2) 强化学习结合科学性反馈奖励（证据收集、工具使用、逻辑非冗余性）训练推理质量。在212个生物医学工具组成的工具宇宙中，智能体每一步识别缺失信息、选择并运行相关工具、整合证据。

Result: 在5个基准测试（3,168个药物推理任务和456个患者治疗案例）中，ATHENA-R1药物推理准确率94.7%，治疗推理准确率82.9%，分别超GPT-5达17.8和10.7个百分点。28个罕见病组织的专家盲评中全面优于参考模型；医生对复杂心血管和感染性疾病案例评估中评价良好。生成的药物不良反应假设在540万患者电子健康记录中验证，调整后比值比达1.48-1.84，阴性对照组无异常。

Conclusion: 治疗推理可被重新定义为一个可学习的迭代证据收集过程，强化学习能够训练AI有效执行这一过程。

Abstract: Treatment reasoning underpins every therapeutic decision, integrating disease context, comorbidities, medications, contraindications, and evolving biomedical knowledge to select an appropriate therapy. It is inherently iterative: candidates are weighed against many constraints, revised as evidence emerges, and grounded in verifiable sources. Here we introduce ATHENA-R1, an AI agent for treatment reasoning across all FDA approved drugs since 1939, trained by reinforcement learning over a universe of 212 biomedical tools. At each step it identifies missing information, selects and runs relevant tools, and incorporates the evidence. To train it without human-annotated traces, we build a two-level self-learning framework: multi-agent systems construct the tools, tasks, and reasoning trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, then reinforcement learning with scientific feedback rewards reasoning quality (evidence gathering, grounded tool use, logical non-redundancy). Across five benchmarks of 3,168 drug reasoning tasks and 456 patient treatment cases, ATHENA-R1 outperforms language models and tool-use systems, reaching 94.7% accuracy on open-ended drug reasoning and 82.9% on treatment reasoning, 17.8 and 10.7 points above GPT-5. In blinded evaluations by experts from 28 rare disease organizations, it is preferred over reference models on all criteria, and physicians rated it favorably on complex hospitalized cardiovascular and infectious-disease cases. Adverse-event hypotheses it generated, tested in electronic health records from 5.4 million patients, reached adjusted odds ratios of 1.48-1.84, with no elevation among negative controls. Because it requires knowing what evidence to seek before concluding, treatment reasoning has long been hard for AI; we show it can be reframed as a learnable process of iterative evidence gathering that reinforcement learning can train AI to perform.

</details>


### [171] [COMPASS: Grounding Composition-Intent Guidance in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28696)
*Ziqi Zhou,Weize Quan,Mining Tan,Zhihan Chen,Dandan Zheng,Jingdong Chen,Jun Zhou,Weiming Dong,Dong-Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COMPASS是首个统一的 multimodal 框架，通过共享专家令牌 τ_c 实现构图感知与构图引导生成的联合控制。在感知端将构图知识注入 MoE 骨干，在生成端用 τ_c 控制去噪过程，配合大规模数据集 Comp-11 训练，显著提升了构图理解与生成的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在细粒度构图识别上不可靠，且无法将构图意图转化为可控生成，缺乏统一的构图感知与生成框架。

Method: 提出 COMPASS 框架：1) 感知端通过 MoE 骨干注入构图专业知识，将推断的构图意图蒸馏为共享专家令牌 τ_c；2) 生成端复用 τ_c 作为全局条件信号指导去噪轨迹；3) 构建包含 11 类分类体系和大规模推理增强标注的 Comp-11 数据集。

Result: 在类别级构图理解任务上显著提升，生成结果在构图一致性和提示忠实度上优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: COMPASS 通过统一的专家令牌机制有效桥接了构图感知与生成，验证了将构图意图作为中心锚点实现端到端可控生成的有效性。

Abstract: Composition is a high-level visual intent that governs where subjects are placed and how a scene is organized, yet current unified multimodal models remain unreliable at fine-grained composition recognition and struggle to turn such intent into controllable generation. We present COMPASS, the first unified multimodal framework that grounds composition-intent control in a single system spanning both composition perception and composition-guided generation, with a shared expert token $τ_c$ as the central intent anchor. On the perception side, COMPASS injects composition expertise into an MoE backbone in a minimally invasive manner and distills the inferred intent into $τ_c$. On the generation side, COMPASS reuses $τ_c$ as a global conditioning signal that steers the denoising trajectory, effectively converting passive composition analysis into explicit layout control. To support systematic instruction-following composition learning and evaluation at scale, we construct Comp-11, a large-scale dataset with an 11-class taxonomy and reasoning-augmented annotations. Extensive experiments show that COMPASS substantially improves category-level composition understanding and delivers more composition-consistent, prompt-faithful generation than strong baselines.

</details>


### [172] [BV-Blend: Uncertainty-Weighted Historical Baselines for Stable Critic-Free RL with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28707)
*Yupeng Chang,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BV-Blend框架，通过结合提示局部奖励统计和语义聚类历史矩来稳定无评论家强化学习的优势估计，解决GRPO在二进制验证器冷启动阶段因组内奖励方差为零导致学习停滞的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等无评论家RLVR方法依赖于提示组内的奖励统计进行优势估计，当组内所有rollout获得相同奖励时，方差为零导致优势为零，学习停滞。作者旨在解决这一冷启动阶段的不稳定性问题。

Method: BV-Blend引入EMA追踪的语义聚类条件历史奖励矩，基于标准误代理计算置信权重，将历史基线/方差与提示局部统计混合，生成标准化优势用于PPO风格的裁剪更新。

Result: 在可验证推理基准上，BV-Blend相比组归一化方法提升了训练稳定性和性能，且在组归一化方法可能停滞的场景下仍保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 通过混合局部和聚类历史统计的无评论家优势估计方法，有效缓解了二进制反馈下的冷启动问题，为无评论家RLVR提供了更稳定的训练范式。

Abstract: Critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), exemplified by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), avoids training a value function (critic) and reduces memory and compute overhead relative to critic-based PPO pipelines for aligning large language models. However, GRPO-style advantage estimation depends on prompt-local (within-prompt-group) reward statistics and can be unstable. In particular, when all rollouts in a prompt group receive identical rewards, the within-group reward variance becomes zero, and group normalization yields zero advantages for that group, impeding learning in cold-start regimes with binary verifiers. We introduce BV-Blend, a critic-free framework that stabilizes advantage estimation by combining prompt-local on-policy statistics with semantic-cluster-conditioned historical moments. BV-Blend maintains EMA-tracked reward moments for each cluster, derives a confidence weight from a standard error of the mean (SEM) proxy, and uses this weight to blend historical and prompt-local baseline and variance statistics into a standardized advantage for PPO-style clipped updates. Experiments on verifiable reasoning benchmarks show that BV-Blend improves training stability and performance, and remains robust in regimes where group-normalized methods may stall.

</details>


### [173] [The Two Genie Game: Adoption and Welfare in Audit-Grounded AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28710)
*Darrell Lewis-Sandy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在负和竞争市场中，采用伤害最小化策略的智能体（自审计智能体）能否取代追求认可的智能体（RLHF智能体），以及该策略是否足以防止社区伤害。通过进化博弈论（Moran-Fermi成对比较）形式化分析，证明了在特定先验分布条件下自审计智能体的采纳优势，并给出了临界采纳水平与社区有效规模N_c的边界定理。同时指出，自审计策略在一般情况下不足以防止社区伤害，其效果取决于审计与社区价值观的对齐程度及评估时间窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在竞争性市场中，采取伤害最小化策略的智能体能否取代传统的RLHF智能体，以及这一策略是否足以保护社区免受伤害，从而为AI安全治理提供理论基础。

Method: 采用有限种群Moran-Fermi成对比较的进化博弈论框架，结合wisher事后认知、同伴证言、单调伤害账本、社区反馈信息密度假设，在负和有限资源环境中进行形式化分析。使用Lean 4进行了代数与有限网格的机器验证。

Result: 1) 当先验分布满足单调性、端点反转和中心对称配对性质时，自审计智能体的采纳受到青睐。2) 存在临界采纳水平，高于该水平则自审计智能体将固定。3) 固定可达的条件是社区有效规模N_c足够小。4) 自审计策略一般不足以防止社区伤害，取决于价值观对齐程度和时间窗口；一旦达到主导地位，状态是吸收性的。

Conclusion: 自审计智能体在特定条件下可取代RLHF智能体，但该策略并非防止社区伤害的充分条件。同一策略在对齐时减少伤害，在未对齐时成为福利陷阱，甚至在对齐时也可能锁定延迟伤害。

Abstract: We ask under what conditions an agent with a harm-minimizing policy can displace an approval-seeking (RLHF) agent in a competitive market, and when that policy is sufficient to prevent community harm. We use evolutionary game theory (finite-population Moran-Fermi pairwise comparison) to formalize this subject to assumptions of wisher hindsight, peer testimony, a monotone harm ledger, sufficient information density of community feedback, and a finite, depleting resource pool, in a negative-sum environment.
  We show that adoption is favored when the prior distributions on how readily wishers attune to community sentiment are monotone, exhibit endpoint inversion, and have a centro-symmetric pairing property, and demonstrate this with several long-tailed priors (Hill, Pareto, Lomax, Frechet). Where it is favored, a critical adoption level separates communities that drift back to the approval-seeking agent from those for which the audited agent fixes; above that level fixation is the overwhelmingly likely outcome. We derive when fixation is attainable as a bound on the effective (informational) size N_c of the community, which must be small enough to allow fixation before depletion. We present these as Theorems 5.4 and 5.5; the algebraic and finite-grid backbone is machine-checked in Lean 4, with the barrier-crossing asymptotics retained as explicit hypotheses.
  We show that a self-audited agent with a community ledger is not, in general, sufficient to prevent community harm. Sufficiency depends both upon the alignment of the agent's audit with community values and the timeframe over which harm is evaluated. Regardless of alignment, once adoption reaches dominance, the state is absorbing. The same policy that reduced harm under alignment becomes a trap, welfare-negative under misalignment and, even under alignment, one that locks in harm deferred past the adoption horizon.

</details>


### [174] [TrajRS: Towards Certified Robustness in Pedestrian Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28716)
*Liang Zhang,Gaojie Jin,Yao Shi,Quanzhi Li,Cheng-Chao Huang,David N. Jansen,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajRS框架，将随机平滑扩展到轨迹预测领域，为平滑后的轨迹预测器提供认证鲁棒半径，并针对"最优预测鲁棒性"和"所有可能预测鲁棒性"两种定义进行形式化保障。实验证明TrajRS能有效为行人轨迹预测器提供鲁棒性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹预测模型的鲁棒性对安全自动驾驶系统至关重要，现有启发式防御策略难以应对复杂对抗攻击，亟需建立可验证的安全保障。

Method: 将传统随机平滑框架扩展为TrajRS，为平滑轨迹预测器提供认证的鲁棒半径；明确并扩展轨迹预测中鲁棒性的形式化定义，分别针对"最优预测鲁棒性"和"所有可能预测鲁棒性"设计具体方案。

Result: 大量实验表明TrajRS能够为所有被研究的平滑行人轨迹预测器有效实现鲁棒性认证。

Conclusion: TrajRS建立了轨迹预测模型可验证的安全保障机制，弥补了启发式防御的不足。

Abstract: The robustness of trajectory prediction models is crucial for developing safe autonomous driving systems. Adversarial attacks on trajectory prediction can significantly impair the accuracy of predicted trajectories, leading to hazardous driving behaviors. While heuristic defense strategies have been implemented to enhance the robustness of trajectory prediction models, these measures often fail against more sophisticated, targeted adversarial attacks. Hence, there is a pressing need to establish verifiable safety assurances for trajectory prediction models. In this paper, we extend the traditional Randomized Smoothing framework to "TrajRS", which provides a certified robust radius for smoothed trajectory predictors. We clarify and expand the formal definitions of robustness in trajectory prediction and tailor the practical TrajRS scheme specifically to "robustness for the optimal prediction" and "robustness for all possible predictions". An extensive set of experiments demonstrates that TrajRS effectively achieves robustness certification for all smoothed pedestrian trajectory predictors in this work.

</details>


### [175] [ComMem: Complementary Memory Systems for Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28719)
*Guanglong Sun,Shuang Cui,Bo Lei,Liyuan Wang,Zihan Zhai,Hongwei Yan,Hang Su,Jun Zhu,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ComMem是一种受大脑互补记忆系统启发的测试时自适应方法，通过快速适应的详细记忆（类似海马体）和慢速整合的抽象记忆（类似新皮层）协同工作，在15个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法要么局部适应而不积累知识，要么在单模态内操作，未能充分利用VLMs的多模态特性。受生物大脑互补记忆系统启发，旨在实现更有效的跨模态测试时自适应。

Method: ComMem包含两个关键组件：1) 类似海马体的快速适应详细记忆——从高置信度测试样本构建动态视觉缓存；2) 类似新皮层的慢速整合抽象记忆——持续优化全局文本原型。每个测试实例联合优化两个记忆系统以保证跨模态一致性。

Result: 在15个基准数据集上，ComMem在自然分布偏移和跨数据集泛化场景下均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: ComMem通过模拟生物大脑的互补记忆系统，有效解决了VLMs测试时自适应中的知识积累和跨模态协同问题，为提升VLMs实际部署的适应性提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) of vision-language models (VLMs) is essential for their robust deployment in dynamic, real-world environments. However, existing TTA methods often adapt locally without accumulating knowledge over time, or operating within a single modality without exploiting VLMs' inherently multi-modal nature. Inspired by the \textbf{Com}plementary \textbf{Mem}ory systems of the biological brain, we propose \textbf{ComMem}, an innovative approach that mimics the distinct but cooperative roles of the hippocampus and neocortex to enable effective TTA for VLMs. ComMem consists of two key components: a fast-adapting detailed memory, akin to the hippocampus, that forms a dynamic visual cache from high-confidence test samples; and a slow-integrating abstract memory, akin to the neocortex, that continually refines global textual prototypes. For each test instance, ComMem jointly optimizes both memory systems to ensure cross-modal consistency. Extensive experiments on 15 benchmark datasets show that ComMem significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods under both natural distribution shifts and cross-dataset generalization, offering a promising direction for enhancing VLMs' practical adaptability.

</details>


### [176] [Agentic Abstention: Do Agents Know When to Stop Instead of Act?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28733)
*Han Luo,Bingbing Wen,Lucy Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了"智能体回避"问题——LLM智能体在多轮交互中应何时停止行动。研究了13个LLM智能体系统和2个智能体框架在28000+任务上的表现，发现主要挑战不仅是能否回避，更是何时回避。提出CONVOLVE方法，通过上下文工程将完整交互轨迹提炼为可复用的停止规则，在WebShop上将及时回避召回率从26.7提升至57.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM回避研究多聚焦于单轮问答中的"回答或回避"决策，但实际LLM智能体在复杂多轮环境中（如网络购物、终端操作）面临的是序列决策问题：智能体可能在不同时刻选择回答、回避或收集更多信息，且回避需求往往在与环境交互后才变得明显。现有系统缺乏对这一问题的系统研究。

Method: 在网页购物、终端环境和问答三个场景下，评估13个LLM作为智能体的系统和2种智能体框架在28000+任务上的表现。提出CONVOLVE（上下文工程方法），将完整交互轨迹蒸馏为可复用的停止规则，在不更新模型参数的前提下改善智能体及时回避的能力。

Result: 1) 主要挑战不仅是能否回避，更是何时回避；2) 部分智能体从不应该回避时回避，另一些则在多次不必要交互后才回避；3) 模型规模、推理能力和智能体框架对回避行为有不同的影响，更大或更强模型有时在及时回避上表现更差；4) CONVOLVE在WebShop上无需更新模型参数即可将Llama-3.3-70B的及时回避召回率从26.7提升至57.4。

Conclusion: 智能体回避是一个重要的序列决策问题，现有系统在及时回避方面存在显著差距。CONVOLVE方法通过上下文工程有效改善了这一问题，为提升LLM智能体在实际部署中的可靠性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: LLM agents are expected to act over multiple turns, using search, browsing interfaces, and terminal tools to complete user goals. Yet not every goal is well specified or achievable in the available environment. In such cases, a reliable agent should recognize that further interaction is unlikely to help and abstain from additional tool calls. We define Agentic Abstention, the problem of deciding when an agent should stop acting under uncertainty. Unlike standard LLM abstention, which is usually evaluated as a single-turn answer-or-abstain decision, agentic abstention is a sequential decision problem: an agent can answer, abstain, or gather more information at each turn, and the need to abstain may only become clear after interacting with the environment. We study this problem across web shopping, terminal environments, and question answering, evaluating 13 LLM-as-agent systems and 2 agent scaffolds on more than 28,000 tasks.
